~~~python
        return next(iter(d))
    if reading == 'cut' and M.cut is None: return UNDEF, None, None, None, None, None
    block, cutf = _block(M, reading)
    s0 = _qn(init if init is not None else M.init)   # the same start representation as the other run entries
    try:
        R = {s0}; fr = [s0]
        while fr:
            s = fr.pop()
            for u in M.alpha:                      # N counts only signal transitions (including rest), not supply cut-off
                t = nxt(s, u)
                if t not in R: R.add(t); fr.append(t)
        N = len(R)
        clampf = lambda a, b: tuple(a[i] if i in M.EXT else v for i, v in enumerate(b))
        m0 = mon[0] if mon else 0
        acc = (lambda m: m in mon[2]) if mon else (lambda m: True)
        mstep = (lambda m, u: mon[1].get((m, u))) if mon else (lambda m, u: 0)
        start = (s0, s0, 0, m0, m0); seen = {start: 0}; Q = deque([start]); Iprime = set(); found = None
        while Q:
            a, b, j, m1, m2 = Q.popleft(); L = seen[(a, b, j, m1, m2)]
            if j > 0:
                Iprime.add(M.proj(b, M.IN))
                if found is None and acc(m1) and acc(m2):
                    ra, rb = a, b
                    for u in ['rest'] * q + [CUT] * (q * cutf):
                        ra, rb = nxt(ra, u), nxt(rb, u); rb = clampf(ra, rb)
                    if M.proj(ra, block) != M.proj(rb, block) and r < 1: found = L   # point distribution: TV = 1 > r
            for u1 in M.alpha:
                for u2 in M.alpha:
                    jj = j + (u1 != u2)
                    if jj > k: continue
                    n1, n2 = mstep(m1, u1), mstep(m2, u2)
                    if n1 is None or n2 is None: continue          # the monitor automaton rejects: this prefix is not in the set of legitimate histories
                    a2 = nxt(a, u1); b2 = clampf(a2, nxt(b, u2))
                    node = (a2, b2, jj, n1, n2)
                    if node not in seen: seen[node] = L + 1; Q.append(node)
    except ValueError:
        return OBS, None, None, None, None, None
    bound = N + N * len(Iprime) * k                       # search-derived bound: N and I′ come from this exhaustive search
    if mon is not None:                                   # Theorem 1 (c): with a monitor automaton of M_c states, multiply by M_c²
        mstates = {mon[0]} | {kk[0] for kk in mon[1]} | set(mon[1].values())
        bound = len(mstates) ** 2 * bound
    return (1 if found is not None else 0), found, N, len(Iprime), bound, len(seen)
def _point(d):
    return next(iter(d)) if len(d) == 1 else None
def probe_class(M, P):
    """Used when "all probes of length ≤ P over the alphabet" is explicitly declared; the adjudicator itself does not make this the default."""
    return [p for pl in range(1, P + 1) for p in itertools.product(M.alpha, repeat=pl)]
def _probes_ok(M, probes, P):
    """𝓟 must be a frozen finite set of probe sequences: each probe nonempty, of length ≤ P, with symbols in the alphabet. Returns None if undeclared."""
    if probes is None: return None
    ps = [tuple(p) for p in probes]
    if any(len(p) == 0 or len(p) > P or any(u not in M.alpha for u in p) for p in ps): return False
    return ps
def _act_on_pair(M, h1, h2, q, r, probes, init, reading='rest', r_act=0.05, mon=None):
    """Traverses only the declared probe class 𝓟 (probes) and enumerates no sequence outside 𝓟; each probe extension h·p must belong to the set of legitimate histories 𝓗, otherwise ⊥_Dom
       (𝓗 constrains input histories; the rest window and measurement tail appended by the protocol are not counted in 𝓗).
       (a) the TV of the act distribution is ≤ r before the probe and > r after it;
       (b) under a non-write probe p (both sides apply p and then rest q steps, and the joint distribution of the compared block has TV ≤ r against that before the application),
           act is a point distribution and the absolute difference of the two sides' increments is > r_act (a numeric predicate; r_act carries the unit of act); act not a point distribution yields ⊥_Obs."""
    rest = ['rest'] * q
    block = M.IN            # clause (b)'s "no write detected" test checks the in-boundary joint distribution per 3.4; both reading modes use all in-boundary components (not narrowed to the declared block of the cut reading)
    # timing (3.4): rest reading = history → rest q → probe → rest q (write check);
    #             cut reading = history → rest q → supply cut-off q → probe → supply cut-off q (write check). The probe continues from the paired state already processed according to the reading mode.
    if reading == 'cut':
        if M.cut is None: return UNDEF
        pre_seq, post_seq = rest + [CUT] * q, [CUT] * q
    else:
        pre_seq, post_seq = rest, rest
    base = run_pair(M, h1 + pre_seq, h2 + pre_seq, init, True)
    if base is None: return OBS
    act = lambda st: _qn(M.act(st))                 # float values of act readings are converted to rationals
    pre1, pre2 = push(base[0], act), push(base[1], act)
    pre = tv(pre1, pre2); unknown = False; undef = False
    for p in probes:
        if not (mon_accepts(mon, h1 + list(p)) and mon_accepts(mon, h2 + list(p))):
            return DOM                             # probe extension h·p not in 𝓗: the declared 𝓟 is illegitimate (the rest window appended by the protocol is not counted in 𝓗, 3.1(3))
        e = run_pair(M, h1 + pre_seq + list(p), h2 + pre_seq + list(p), init, True)
        if e is None: unknown = True; continue
        post = tv(push(e[0], act), push(e[1], act))
        if pre <= r and post > r: return 1
        w = run_pair(M, h1 + pre_seq + list(p) + post_seq, h2 + pre_seq + list(p) + post_seq, init, True)
        if w is None: unknown = True; continue
        if diff_block(M, base[0], w[0], block) > r or diff_block(M, base[1], w[1], block) > r:
            continue                           # write probe: clause (b) does not count it
        a1, a2, b1, b2 = _point(pre1), _point(pre2), _point(push(e[0], act)), _point(push(e[1], act))
        if None in (a1, a2, b1, b2): unknown = True; continue
        if not all(isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)) and not isinstance(v, bool) for v in (a1, a2, b1, b2)):
            undef = True; continue                 # act not declared numeric (3.1(6)): clause (b) cannot be adjudicated
        if abs(_q(b1) - _q(a1) - (_q(b2) - _q(a2))) > r_act: return 1
    return UNDEF if undef else (OBS if unknown else 0)
@_guard(1)
def det_act(M, q=6, r=0.05, k=1, H=3, P=2, reading='rest', init=None, r_act=0.05, mon=None, probes=None):
    """probes: the frozen probe class 𝓟 (a finite set of probe sequences). Undeclared yields ⊥_Undef; the empty set is legitimate and, having no witness, yields 0."""
    e = validate_layer1_decl(M, q=q, k=k, H=H, r=r, thresholds=(r_act,), mon=mon, reading=reading, init=init)
    if e: return e
    r, r_act = _q(r), _q(r_act)
    ps = _probes_ok(M, probes, P)
    if ps is None: return UNDEF
    if ps is False: return DOM
    # legitimacy of 𝓟 is a global declaration-level requirement (3.1(3)(6)): for every legitimate history h in the search domain and every p ∈ 𝓟, h·p ∈ 𝓗 must hold.
    # it is checked before the witness search, so the 0 of "no witness" cannot mask it.
    if mon is not None and ps:
        for L in range(1, H + 1):
            for h in itertools.product(M.alpha, repeat=L):
                if mon_accepts(mon, list(h)) and not all(mon_accepts(mon, list(h) + list(p)) for p in ps): return DOM
    g, st = witnesses(M, q, r, k, H, reading, init, mon=mon)
    unknown = st['obs']
    for h1, h2 in g:
        v = _act_on_pair(M, h1, h2, q, r, ps, init, reading, r_act, mon)
        if v == 1: return 1
        if v == DOM: return DOM
        if v == OBS: unknown = True
        if v == UNDEF: return UNDEF
    return OBS if (unknown or st['obs']) else 0
def _ref(h_act, seg): return [('rest' if i in set(seg) else u) for i, u in enumerate(h_act)]   # seg has passed the entry check (type, bounds, no duplicates)
@_guard(1)
def det_hist(M, h_act, seg, q=6, r=0.05, reading='rest', init=None, mon=None):
    e = validate_layer1_decl(M, q=q, r=r, mon=mon, reading=reading, init=init)
    if e: return e
    r = _q(r)
    if h_act is None: return OBS               # no record
    e = validate_layer1_decl(M, hists=(h_act,), seg=seg, seg_len=len(h_act) if isinstance(h_act, (list, tuple)) else 0)
    if e: return e
    h_act = list(h_act)                         # after the legitimacy check, convert to a list; tuples and lists are equivalent
    if not (mon_accepts(mon, h_act) and mon_accepts(mon, _ref(h_act, seg))): return DOM   # actual history or reference history not in 𝓗
    block, cutf = _block(M, reading)
    res = run_pair(M, h_act + ['rest'] * q, _ref(h_act, seg) + ['rest'] * q, init, True, ncut=q * cutf)
    if res is None: return OBS
    return 1 if diff_block(M, *res, block) > r else 0
@_guard(1)
def det_act_h(M, h_act, seg, q=6, r=0.05, P=2, reading='rest', init=None, r_act=0.05, probes=None, mon=None):
    e = validate_layer1_decl(M, q=q, r=r, thresholds=(r_act,), mon=mon, reading=reading, init=init)
    if e: return e
    r, r_act = _q(r), _q(r_act)
    ps = _probes_ok(M, probes, P)
    if ps is None: return UNDEF
    if ps is False: return DOM
    if h_act is None: return OBS
    e = validate_layer1_decl(M, hists=(h_act,), seg=seg, seg_len=len(h_act) if isinstance(h_act, (list, tuple)) else 0)
    if e: return e
    h_act = list(h_act)                         # after the legitimacy check, convert to a list; tuples and lists are equivalent
    h_ref = _ref(h_act, seg)
    if not (mon_accepts(mon, h_act) and mon_accepts(mon, h_ref)): return DOM
    # probe legitimacy is checked before Hist and witnesses: both h·p and h̄·p must belong to 𝓗
    if not all(mon_accepts(mon, h_act + list(p)) and mon_accepts(mon, h_ref + list(p)) for p in ps): return DOM
    h = det_hist(M, h_act, seg, q, r, reading, init, mon)
    if h != 1: return h
    return _act_on_pair(M, h_act, h_ref, q, r, ps, init, reading, r_act, mon)
def y_effect(M, s, x, y, a, b, u='rest'):
    """Dep effect (Δμ, ρ): the pre-state, setting level and post-state readings are each first converted to exact rationals and then subtracted; the values, probabilities and mean of the change distribution,
       the translation keys and the residual stay rational throughout, with no floating point."""
    def dx(val):
        st = list(_qn(tuple(s))); st[y] = _qn(val); st = tuple(st)
        return push(M.step(st, u), lambda z: _qn(z[x]) - st[x])
    A, B = dx(a), dx(b)
    mA = sum(k * p for k, p in A.items()); mB = sum(k * p for k, p in B.items()); dm = mB - mA
    return dm, tv(B, {k + dm: p for k, p in A.items()})
import builtins as _bi, sys as _sys
def _code_canon(c):
    return (c.co_code, tuple(_code_canon(k) if hasattr(k, 'co_code') else repr(k) for k in c.co_consts),
            c.co_names, c.co_varnames, c.co_argcount, c.co_posonlyargcount, c.co_kwonlyargcount, c.co_flags)
def _code_names(c):
    out = set(c.co_names)
    for k in c.co_consts:
        if hasattr(k, 'co_code'): out |= _code_names(k)
    return out
def _member_id(domain):
    """Frozen identity of a membership test on an infinite setting domain: (declared identifier, implementation digest, interpreter version).
    The digest covers bytecode, constants, names, parameter shape (numbers of positional, positional-only and keyword-only parameters, and flags), closure values, positional defaults and keyword-only defaults; repeated declarations of the same implementation give the same digest, and different implementations give different digests;
    when the member references global names other than built-ins, the digest cannot cover those global values and an identifier must be declared separately; otherwise returns None (identity undeterminable)."""
    member = domain[1]; ident = domain[2] if len(domain) > 2 else None
    code = getattr(member, '__code__', None)
    if code is None: return None if ident is None else (ident, None, _sys.version_info[:2])
    ext = {n for n in _code_names(code) if not hasattr(_bi, n)}
    if ext and ident is None: return None
    clos = tuple(repr(cell.cell_contents) for cell in (member.__closure__ or ()))
    kwd = tuple(sorted((k, repr(v)) for k, v in (getattr(member, '__kwdefaults__', None) or {}).items()))
    canon = repr((_code_canon(code), clos, repr(member.__defaults__), kwd))
    return (ident, hashlib.sha256(canon.encode()).hexdigest()[:16], _sys.version_info[:2])
@_guard(1)
def det_dep(M, h_act, seg, x, y, levels, r_mu=0.05, r_rho=0.05, init=None, u='rest', domain=None):
    """levels: the setting levels the program actually enumerates. domain: the declared setting domain V_y —
       by default, levels itself (a frozen finite discrete level set, fully enumerated);
       if a finite set, it must equal levels, otherwise ⊥_Dom;
       if ('infinite', member) (a continuous interval or an infinite set), member(a) declares whether level a belongs to V_y and its setting is legitimate:
       every measured level must pass member, otherwise ⊥_Dom; a witness yields 1; no witness gives only in-range 0 and reports the actual subdomain: (IN0, measured levels);
       a declaration that writes only 'infinite' without a membership test yields ⊥_Dom.
       One may also write ('infinite', member, identifier), where the identifier is the declared protocol name. The identity of the membership test is given by _member_id (identifier and implementation digest);
       if member references global names other than built-ins and no identifier is declared, the identity is undeterminable and the result is ⊥_Dom."""
    e = validate_layer1_decl(M, thresholds=(r_mu, r_rho), init=init, idx=(x, y))
    if e: return e
    r_mu, r_rho = _q(r_mu), _q(r_rho)
    inf = isinstance(domain, tuple) and len(domain) in (2, 3) and domain[0] == 'infinite'
    if domain == 'infinite' or (inf and not callable(domain[1])): return DOM
    levels = [_qn(a) for a in levels]              # setting levels and a finite V_y are compared in the same effective representation (3.3)
    if domain is not None and not inf: domain = [_qn(a) for a in domain]
    if inf and _member_id(domain) is None: return DOM          # identity of the membership test undeterminable: cannot serve as a frozen declaration
    if inf and not all(domain[1](a) for a in levels): return DOM
    if domain is not None and not inf and set(domain) != set(levels): return DOM
    if u not in M.alpha: return DOM
    if h_act is None: return OBS
    e = validate_layer1_decl(M, hists=(h_act,), seg=seg, seg_len=len(h_act) if isinstance(h_act, (list, tuple)) else 0)
    if e: return e
    h_act = list(h_act)                         # after the legitimacy check, convert to a list; tuples and lists are equivalent
    res = run_pair(M, h_act, _ref(h_act, seg), init, True)
    if res is None: return OBS
    s, sb = _point(res[0]), _point(res[1])
    if s is None or sb is None: return OBS    # a random terminal state yields ⊥_Obs (Section 3.4, Dep)
    for a, b in itertools.combinations(levels, 2):
        e1, e2 = y_effect(M, s, x, y, a, b, u), y_effect(M, sb, x, y, a, b, u)
        if abs(e1[0] - e2[0]) > r_mu or abs(e1[1] - e2[1]) > r_rho: return 1
    return (IN0, tuple(sorted(levels))) if inf else 0
# ---------------- 4.1 five-tuple report adapter ----------------
from collections import namedtuple
Det = namedtuple('Det', 'v Code Level Dom Read')
import inspect, hashlib
def _mon_digest(mon):
    """Frozen identifier of the monitor automaton: if undeclared, the full sequence language; if declared, the number of states, the number of transitions, the accepting states and the SHA-256 digest (first 12 hex digits)."""
    if mon is None: return 'full sequence language'
    m0, delta, acc = mon
    canon = repr((m0, sorted(delta.items(), key=repr), sorted(acc, key=repr)))
    states = {m0} | {k[0] for k in delta} | set(delta.values())
    return f'monitor automaton ({len(states)} states, {len(delta)} transitions, accepting states {sorted(acc, key=repr)}, digest {hashlib.sha256(canon.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]})'
def report(kind, M, *args, **kw):
    """Call a first-layer adjudicator and write the result as the five-tuple Det(v, Code, Level, Dom, Read) of 4.1.
    kind ∈ {'Tr', 'Tr_exact', 'Hist', 'Act', 'Act_h', 'Dep'}; parameters as for the corresponding adjudicator.
    v ∈ {1, 0, '⊥'}; Code: for 1, "witness"; for 0, "in-range 0" (holds only for the declared search bound, 𝓟 or measured levels) or "full-graph search 0"; for ⊥, the reason code;
    Level is Y₁; Dom is the adjudication mode, coupling protocol, 𝓗 and C_op; Read is the witness, the history length and search bound, or the measured subdomain."""
    fn = {'Tr': det_tr_enum, 'Tr_exact': det_tr_exact, 'Hist': det_hist, 'Act': det_act, 'Act_h': det_act_h, 'Dep': det_dep}[kind]
    bound = inspect.signature(fn).bind(M, *args, **kw)     # positional arguments, keyword arguments and defaults are bound uniformly into the effective configuration
    bound.apply_defaults()
    prm = dict(bound.arguments)
    out = fn(M, *args, **kw)
    read = None
    if kind == 'Tr': v, read = out[0], {'witness': out[1]}
    elif kind == 'Tr_exact': v, read = out[0], {'shortest history length': out[1], 'N': out[2], "|I'|": out[3], 'search-derived upper bound on history length': out[4], 'expanded nodes searched': out[5],
                                                 'a priori history-length bound': 'not provided (upper bounds on N and on the in-boundary state space |S_in| must be declared, Theorem 1 (a2))'}
    elif isinstance(out, tuple) and out and out[0] == IN0: v, read = IN0, {'measured subdomain': out[1]}
    else: v = out
    if v == 1: val, code = 1, 'witness'
    elif v == 0: val, code = 0, ('full-graph search 0 (for every legitimate finite history)' if kind == 'Tr_exact' else 'in-range 0 (only for the declared search bound, 𝓟 and level set)')
    elif v == IN0: val, code = 0, 'in-range 0 (only for the measured subdomain)'
    else: val, code = '⊥', v
    cop = {}
    for key in ('q', 'r', 'k', 'H', 'P', 'r_act', 'r_mu', 'r_rho', 'reading', 'probes', 'levels', 'domain', 'h_act', 'seg', 'x', 'y', 'u', 'clamp'):
        if key not in prm: continue
        x = prm[key]
        if key == 'probes' and x is not None: x = tuple(tuple(p) for p in x)
        if key == 'domain' and isinstance(x, tuple) and len(x) in (2, 3) and x[0] == 'infinite':
            mid = _member_id(x)
            x = ('infinite', 'membership-test identity undeterminable') if mid is None else ('infinite', {'identifier': mid[0], 'implementation digest': mid[1], 'interpreter': '%d.%d' % mid[2]})
        if key == 'seg' and x is not None: x = tuple(sorted(x))
        if key == 'h_act' and isinstance(x, (list, tuple)): x = tuple(x)       # lists and tuples recorded as the same configuration
        cop[key] = x
    dom = {'adjudication mode': 'model mode', 'coupling protocol': 'twin protocol (disturbance shared within a beat, independent beat by beat)' if M.struct is not None else 'value clamping (transition kernel, baseline out-of-boundary a point distribution)',
           '𝓗': _mon_digest(prm.get('mon')), 'start s₀': _qn(prm.get('init')) if prm.get('init') is not None else M.init, 'C_op': cop}
    return Det(val, code, 'Y₁', dom, read)
clip = lambda v, lo, hi: max(lo, min(hi, v))
_G_FLAG = True
def _member_global(a): return _G_FLAG     # the membership test reads a module global value: the digest cannot cover it, so an identifier must be declared
# ---------------- Models ----------------
def echo():
    return Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda s, u: {(clip(s[0] // 2 + (2 if u == 'k' else 0), 0, 4),): 1.0}, (0,), ['k', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[0])
def correlated_bits():
    def step(s, u):
        a, b = s
        if u == 'eq': return {(0, 0): .5, (1, 1): .5}
        if u == 'ne': return {(0, 1): .5, (1, 0): .5}
        return {s: 1.0}
    return Model(['a', 'b'], [1, 1], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['eq', 'ne', 'rest'])
def recode(M, f, finv, medium, inside):
    return Model(M.names, medium, inside, lambda s, u: push(M.step(finv(s), u), f), f(M.init), M.alpha,
                 lambda s: M.act(finv(s)), lambda s: push(M.cut(finv(s)), f))
def conservative_pair():
    return Model(['X1', 'X2'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda s, u: {((s[0] + 1, s[1] - 1) if (u == 'w' and s[1] > 0) else s): 1.0}, (0, 2), ['w', 'rest'])
def mirror():
    return Model(['A', 'B'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda s, u: {((1, 1) if u == 'w' else s): 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
def mutual_external():
    def step(s, u):
        m, E = s; m2, E2 = E, m
        if u == 'w': m2 = 1
        return {(m2, E2): 1.0}
    return Model(['m', 'E'], [1, 1], [1, 0], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'], lambda s: s[0])
def noisy_external():  # the out-of-boundary component takes random values at rest: clamping cannot be realized → ⊥_Obs
    def step(s, u):
        m, E = s; m2 = 1 if u == 'w' else E
        return {(m2, 1): .5, (m2, 0): .5} if u == 'rest' else {(m2, m2): 1.0}
    return Model(['m', 'E'], [1, 1], [1, 0], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
def noisy_external_struct():  # as above, with a declared structural representation: at rest the out-of-boundary component takes the exogenous disturbance w; adjudicable under the twin protocol
    def phi(s, u, w):
        m, E = s; m2 = 1 if u == 'w' else E
        return (m2, w) if u == 'rest' else (m2, m2)
    return Model(['m', 'E'], [1, 1], [1, 0], None, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(phi, {0: .5, 1: .5}))
def null_signal_struct():  # counterexample of Section 3.3: the signal enters no variable; in-boundary m, p, y, out-of-boundary E
    def phi(s, u, w):
        m, p, y, E = s
        return (w, m, p ^ E, m)
    return Model(['m', 'p', 'y', 'E'], [1, 1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1, 0], None, (0, 0, 0, 0), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(phi, {0: .5, 1: .5}))
def hybrid_struct(C=12):  # structural representation of M1 (trivial disturbance): the twin protocol agrees with value clamping, and the mixed-state witness appears as usual
    base = hybrid(C)
    return Model(['m', 'E'], [1, 1], [1, 0], None, (0, 0), ['w', 'a', 'rest'],
                 struct=(lambda s, u, w: next(iter(base.step(s, u))), {0: 1.0}))
def shape_model():  # Dep two thresholds: w sets z = 1; Δx = y when z = 0, Δx = 4y·B when z = 1 (B a fair coin)
    def step(s, u):
        x, y, z = s
        if u == 'w': return {(x, y, 1): 1.0}
        if u == 'p':
            if z == 0: return {(x + y, y, z): 1.0}
            return push({0: .5, 1: .5}, lambda b: (x + 4 * y * b, y, z))   # when y = 0 the two branches merge into one successor; the probabilities still sum to 1
        return {s: 1.0}
    return Model(['x', 'y', 'z'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], step, (0, 0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'])
def null_signal_noext():  # only in-boundary m, m_{t+1} = ω: no out-of-boundary component, clamping has no effect
    return Model(['m'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(lambda s, u, w: (w,), {0: .5, 1: .5}))
def gate_once():  # variant of mirrored storage: a is a no-op, used to test the legitimate-history constraint of the monitor automaton
    return Model(['A', 'B'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda s, u: {((1, 1) if u == 'w' else s): 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'a', 'rest'])
MON_NO_W = (0, {(0, 'a'): 0, (0, 'rest'): 0}, {0})                          # 𝓗: all histories without w
MON_W_AFTER_A = (0, {(0, 'a'): 1, (0, 'rest'): 0, (1, 'a'): 1, (1, 'rest'): 1, (1, 'w'): 1}, {0, 1})   # 𝓗: w may appear only after some a
def hybrid(C=12):  # clamped mixed state: under normal dynamics m≡E (N=2); after clamping m≠E, and a increments m up to C
    def step(s, u):
        m, E = s
        if u == 'w': return {(1, 1): 1.0}
        if m != E:
            if u == 'a': return {(min(C, m + 1), E): 1.0}
            if u == 'rest' and m < C: return {(E, E): 1.0}
        return {(m, E): 1.0}
    return Model(['m', 'E'], [1, 1], [1, 0], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'a', 'rest'])
TAB = {((0, 0), 'a'): (0, 0), ((0, 0), 'b'): (1, 0), ((0, 0), 'rest'): (0, 0), ((0, 1), 'a'): (0, 1), ((0, 1), 'b'): (0, 0),
       ((0, 1), 'rest'): (0, 0), ((1, 0), 'a'): (0, 1), ((1, 0), 'b'): (0, 0), ((1, 0), 'rest'): (0, 1), ((1, 1), 'a'): (1, 0),
       ((1, 1), 'b'): (0, 1), ((1, 1), 'rest'): (1, 1)}
def table_model():  # counterexample in which the position-count bound fails when there are out-of-boundary variables (found by random search)
    return Model(['m', 'E'], [1, 1], [1, 0], lambda s, u: {TAB[(s, u)]: 1.0}, (0, 0), ['a', 'b', 'rest'])
def gated_clock(G=4, eps=0.04):
    def step(s, u):
        c, x = s; c2 = (c + 1) % G
        if u == 'w' and c == 0: return {(c2, clip(x + 1, 0, 3)): eps, (c2, x): 1 - eps}
        return {(c2, x): 1.0}
    return Model(['clock', 'x'], [0, 1], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
def prob_write(eps=0.05):
    return Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda s, u: ({(1,): eps, (0,): 1 - eps} if (u == 'w' and s[0] == 0) else {s: 1.0}), (0,), ['w', 'rest'])
def slow_leak(p=0.02):
    def step(s, u):
        if u == 'w': return {(1,): 1.0}
        if s[0] == 1: return {(1,): 1 - p, (0,): p}
        return {s: 1.0}
    return Model(['charge'], [1], [1], step, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], cut=lambda st: step(st, 'rest'))  # leakage is independent of supply
def long_prep(n=6):  # requires n consecutive preparations a; rest or a premature write resets the count
    def step(s, u):
        c, x = s
        if u == 'a': c = min(n, c + 1)
        elif u == 'w' and c >= n: x = 1
        else: c = 0
        return {(c, x): 1.0}
    return Model(['prep', 'x'], [0, 1], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['a', 'w', 'rest'], cut=lambda st: {(0, st[1]): 1.0})
def fuse_rom():
    return Model(['bit', 'lock'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda s, u: {((1, 1) if (u == 'w' and not s[1]) else s): 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'],
                 act=lambda s: 0, cut=lambda st: {st: 1.0})
def refresh_dram():  # refresh (energy supply) maintains the charge; lost on supply cut-off
    return Model(['charge'], [1], [1], lambda s, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else s): 1.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[0],
                 cut=lambda s: {(0,): 1.0})
def receiver_saturation():
    K = 3
    def step(s, u):
        x, y = s
        if u == 'w': y = clip(y + 1, 0, 2)
        if u == 'a': x = clip(x + 1, 0, K)
        if u == 'p' and y > 0 and x < K: x = x + 1
        return {(x, y): 1.0}
    return Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'a', 'p', 'rest'], lambda s: s[0])
def spontaneous_channel():  # groove depth y (declared block) shapes the spontaneous flow o at rest; under inflow p the flow is 2y+1
    def step(s, u):
        y, o = s
        if u == 'w': y = clip(y + 1, 0, 2)
        o = 2 * y + 1 if u == 'p' else y
        return {(y, o): 1.0}
    return Model(['depth', 'flow'], [1, 0], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[1],
                 cut=lambda st: {(st[0], 0): 1.0})
def recording(M, seen):  # wrapper model: records every input symbol actually applied during adjudication
    def step(st, u):
        seen.add(u); return M.step(st, u)
    return Model(M.names, [1 if i in M.MED else 0 for i in range(len(M.names))], [1 if i in M.IN else 0 for i in range(len(M.names))],
                 step, M.init, M.alpha, M.act, M.cut)
def dep_unsampled():  # finite sampling for Dep: w sets z = 1; at rest Δx = 1 if and only if z = 1 and y ≥ 3, so there is no difference anywhere on the levels {0, 1, 2}
    def step(s, u):
        x, y, z = s
        if u == 'w': return {(x, y, 1): 1.0}
        return {(x + (1 if (z == 1 and y >= 3) else 0), y, z): 1.0}
    return Model(['x', 'y', 'z'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], step, (0, 0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
def selfread():  # act is the retained state itself; writes saturate. Act_𝓟 = 0
    return Model(['y'], [1], [1], lambda s, u: {((min(2, s[0] + 1),) if u == 'w' else s): 1.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[0])
def usage_ltp():  # read-out potentiates: on probe p the output o = y+1 and y increases by one; at rest o = 0
    def step(s, u):
        y, o = s
        if u == 'w': return {(min(3, y + 1), 0): 1.0}
        if u == 'p': return {(min(3, y + 1), y + 1): 1.0}
        return {(y, 0): 1.0}
    return Model(['y', 'o'], [1, 0], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda s: s[1])
def linear_additive():
    return Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda s, u: {(clip(s[0] + s[1], -30, 30), clip(s[1] + (1 if u == 'w' else 0), 0, 2)): 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
def tests():
    T = []
    add = lambda name, got, exp: T.append((name, got, exp, '✓' if got == exp else '✗'))
    add('Echo Tr (exact)', det_tr_exact(echo())[0], 0)
    # Lemma 1 (metric): correlated states
    M = correlated_bits()
    add('Correlated bits joint-TV Tr', det_tr_enum(M, q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], 1)
    marg = max(tv(push(d1, lambda s: s[i]), push(d2, lambda s: s[i])) for i in (0, 1)
               for d1, d2 in [run_pair(M, ['eq'], ['ne'])])
    add('Correlated bits max single-variable marginal difference > r?', int(marg > 0.05), 0)
    f = lambda s: (s[0], s[0] ^ s[1])
    add('Correlated bits Tr after block re-encoding', det_tr_enum(recode(M, f, f, [1, 1], [1, 1]), q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], 1)
    add('Conserved pair Tr', det_tr_exact(conservative_pair())[0], 1)
    add('Mirrored storage Tr', det_tr_exact(mirror())[0], 1)
    # Lemma 2
    M = mutual_external()
    add('Mutual write-back to external store, unclamped Tr', det_tr_enum(M, q=4, k=1, H=2, clamp=False)[0], 1)
    add('Mutual write-back to external store, clamped Tr (exact)', det_tr_exact(M, q=4, k=1)[0], 0)
    add('Mutual write-back to external store Act_𝓟', det_act(M, q=2, H=2, P=1, probes=probe_class(M, 1)), 0)
    # random clamping: supported domain for transition kernels, and the twin protocol
    add('Random out-of-boundary (transition kernel only) Tr → ⊥_Obs', det_tr_enum(noisy_external(), q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], OBS)
    add('Random out-of-boundary (declared structural representation) adjudicable under twin protocol: the difference passes only through the out-of-boundary component and clamping eliminates it, Tr', det_tr_enum(noisy_external_struct(), q=2, k=1, H=2)[0], 0)
    M = null_signal_struct()
    a, b = run_twin(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['w', 'rest', 'rest'])
    a2, b2 = run_twin(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], shared=False)
    yb = lambda d: push(d, lambda st: st[2])
    # two mathematical objects kept separate: the TV of the marginals, and the disagreement probability Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂) on the paired joint distribution
    def dis(J): return round(float(sum(p for (s1, s2), p in J.items() if s1[2] != s2[2])), 4)
    hh = ['w', 'rest', 'rest']
    add('Signal does not enter the system, same history, shared disturbance: marginal TV of y / Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂) (analytic: 0 / 0)',
        (round(float(tv(yb(a), yb(b))), 4), dis(twin_joint(M, hh, hh))), (0.0, 0.0))
    add('Signal does not enter the system, same history, independent sampling: marginal TV of y / Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂) (analytic: 0.5 / 0.5; baseline side y ≡ 0, control side y = m_ctl ⊕ m_base uniform)',
        (round(float(tv(yb(a2), yb(b2))), 4), dis(twin_joint(M, hh, hh, shared=False))), (0.5, 0.5))
    a3, b3 = run_twin(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['rest', 'rest', 'rest'])
    add('Signal does not enter the system, different histories, shared disturbance: marginal TV of y / Pr(y₁ ≠ y₂) (analytic: 0 / 0)',
        (round(float(tv(yb(a3), yb(b3))), 4), dis(twin_joint(M, ['w', 'rest', 'rest'], ['rest', 'rest', 'rest']))), (0.0, 0.0))
    Mn = null_signal_noext()
    j1 = twin_joint(Mn, hh, hh, shared=False); m1_, m2_ = push(j1, lambda ab: ab[0][0]), push(j1, lambda ab: ab[1][0])
    add('No out-of-boundary component (unclamped), same history, independent sampling: marginal TV / Pr(the two sides disagree) (analytic: 0 / 0.5 — without overwriting, the marginals do not depend on the coupling)',
        (round(float(tv(m1_, m2_)), 4), round(float(sum(p for (s1, s2), p in j1.items() if s1[0] != s2[0])), 4)), (0.0, 0.5))
    add('Signal does not enter the system: Tr under twin protocol', det_tr_enum(M, q=3, k=1, H=2)[0], 0)
    M = hybrid_struct(3)
    w1 = det_tr_enum(M, q=2, k=1, H=4); w2 = det_tr_enum(hybrid(3), q=2, k=1, H=4)
    add('Clamped mixed state (C = 3, structural representation): twin protocol and value clamping give the same verdict and witness', (w1[0], w1[1] == w2[1]), (1, True))
    # resolution threshold
    add('Valid domain of resolution threshold: mirrored storage r = 0.05 yields 1; r = 1, r = −0.1 fall outside Dom2 and yield ⊥_Dom (enumeration and exact)',
        (det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=0.05)[0], det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=1)[0], det_tr_enum(mirror(), r=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=-0.1)[0]), (1, DOM, DOM, DOM))
    add('Resolution threshold r = 0 is valid: mirrored storage yields 1', det_tr_exact(mirror(), r=0)[0], 1)
    # set of legitimate histories declared by a monitor automaton (Theorem 1 (c))
    M = gate_once()
    add('Full sequence language has a witness; with 𝓗 forbidding w, no witness (exact and enumeration)',
        (det_tr_exact(M, q=1)[0], det_tr_exact(M, q=1, mon=MON_NO_W)[0], det_tr_enum(M, q=1, H=3, mon=MON_NO_W)[0]), (1, 0, 0))
    g1 = det_tr_exact(M, q=1); g2 = det_tr_exact(M, q=1, mon=MON_W_AFTER_A); g3 = det_tr_enum(M, q=1, H=3, mon=MON_W_AFTER_A)
    add('𝓗 requires w after a: the shortest witness over the full sequence language (history length 1) is forbidden, the shortest legitimate witness has history length 2, the enumerated witness is legitimate',
        (g1[1], g2[0], g2[1], g3[0], mon_accepts(MON_W_AFTER_A, g3[1][0]) and mon_accepts(MON_W_AFTER_A, g3[1][1])), (1, 1, 2, 1, True))
    # Theorem 1
    g, L, N, I, B, _ = det_tr_exact(hybrid(), q=2, k=1)
    add(f'Clamped mixed state exact Tr (L={L}, N={N}, N²(k+1)={N*N*2}, |I′|={I}, bound={B})', (g, L <= B, L > N * N * 2), (1, True, True))
    for rd in ('cut', 'rest'):
        M = long_prep(6)
        add(f'Long preparatory history, enumeration H=6 Tr^{rd} (in-range 0: holds only for H_max=6)', det_tr_enum(M, q=1, k=1, H=6, reading=rd)[0], 0)
        g, L, N, I, B, _ = det_tr_exact(M, q=1, k=1, reading=rd)
        add(f'Long preparatory history, exact Tr^{rd} (full-graph search, L={L}, N={N}, bound={B}; recorded separately from in-range 0)', (g, L), (1, 7))
    # Theorem 2 and the out-of-boundary counterexample
    add('Gated clock k=3 positions (H=9)', det_tr_enum(gated_clock(), q=1, k=3, H=9)[0], 1)
    add('Random subthreshold k=1 (r=0.08)', det_tr_enum(prob_write(.05), q=1, r=.08, k=1, H=3)[0], 0)
    add('Random subthreshold k=2 (r=0.08)', det_tr_enum(prob_write(.05), q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=3)[0], 1)
    # Theorem 4: the first-layer verdict is a functional of (Prof, C_op). Same Prof (same random-write transition kernel); changing only r or k in C_op flips the verdict
    Mp = prob_write(.05)
    add('Same Prof, change r in C_op (H_max=1, k=1): r = 0.01 yields 1, r = 0.08 yields 0',
        (det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.01, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=1, H=1)[0]), (1, 0))
    add('Same Prof, change k in C_op (H_max=2, r=0.08): k = 1 yields 0, k = 2 yields 1 ((w,w) vs (rest,rest), TV = 0.0975)',
        (det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=1, H=2)[0], det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=2)[0]), (0, 1))
    add('Same Prof, same C_op: same verdict (two independently constructed models)',
        det_tr_enum(prob_write(.05), q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=2)[0] == det_tr_enum(Mp, q=1, r=.08, k=2, H=2)[0], True)
    M = table_model()
    add('Out-of-boundary counterexample, clamped k=1', det_tr_exact(M, q=1, k=1)[0], 0)
    add('Out-of-boundary counterexample, clamped k=2', det_tr_exact(M, q=1, k=2)[0], 1)
    add('Out-of-boundary model, unclamped k=2 (consistent with Theorem 2 (a): no witness for k=1, 2)', (det_tr_enum(M, q=1, k=1, H=4, clamp=False)[0], det_tr_enum(M, q=1, k=2, H=4, clamp=False)[0]), (0, 0))
    # Lemma 5
    add('Medium leakage q=6 Tr^rest', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=6, k=1, H=1)[0], 1)
    add('Medium leakage q=300 Tr^rest', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=300, k=1, H=1)[0], 0)
    add('Medium leakage q=6 Tr^cut (declared cut-off law)', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=6, k=1, H=1, reading='cut')[0], 1)
    add('Medium leakage q=300 Tr^cut', det_tr_enum(slow_leak(), q=300, k=1, H=1, reading='cut')[0], 0)
    add('Supply cut-off undeclared Tr^cut → ⊥_Undef', det_tr_exact(mirror(), reading='cut')[0], UNDEF)
    # supply cut-off test
    M = refresh_dram()
    add('Refresh-maintained charge Tr^rest', det_tr_exact(M, q=2, reading='rest')[0], 1)
    add('Refresh-maintained charge Tr^cut', det_tr_exact(M, q=2, reading='cut')[0], 0)
    # fuse ROM: start and record
    M = fuse_rom()
    add('Fuse ROM from blank has a record Hist^cut/Hist^rest', (det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='cut', init=(0, 0)),
                                             det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='rest', init=(0, 0))), (1, 1))
    add('Fuse ROM from burned state has no record Hist^rest', det_hist(M, None, set(), q=2, init=(1, 1)), OBS)
    # Act: spontaneous activity
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    base = run_pair(M, ['w', 'rest'], ['rest', 'rest'])
    add('Spontaneous flow channel act already differs before the probe', int(tv(push(base[0], M.act), push(base[1], M.act)) > .05), 1)
    add('Spontaneous flow channel Act_𝓟 (clause (b) increment criterion)', det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, probes=probe_class(M, 1)), 1)
    add('Receiver saturation Act_𝓟', det_act(receiver_saturation(), q=1, H=2, P=1, probes=probe_class(receiver_saturation(), 1)), 1)
    add('Self-read saturation (act is the retained state, probe is a write) Act_𝓟', det_act(selfread(), q=1, k=2, H=2, P=1, probes=probe_class(selfread(), 1)), 0)
    add('Read-out potentiation Act_𝓟 (via clause (a))', det_act(usage_ltp(), q=1, H=1, P=1, probes=probe_class(usage_ltp(), 1)), 1)
    # Dep
    add('Receiver saturation Dep (read at p)', det_dep(receiver_saturation(), ['a', 'a', 'a'], {0, 1, 2}, 0, 1, [0, 1], u='p'), 1)
    M = receiver_saturation()
    res = run_pair(M, ['a', 'a', 'a'], ['rest', 'rest', 'rest'])
    s1, s2 = _point(res[0]), _point(res[1])
    e1, e2 = y_effect(M, s1, 0, 1, 0, 1, 'p'), y_effect(M, s2, 0, 1, 0, 1, 'p')
    dmu, drho = abs(e1[0] - e2[0]), abs(e1[1] - e2[1])
    add(f'Receiver saturation, differences of the two effects (|Δμ diff| = {dmu}, |ρ diff| = {drho})', (dmu > 0, drho >= 0), (True, True))
    add('Dep two thresholds: r_μ just above |Δμ diff|, r_ρ above |ρ diff| → 0; r_μ lowered below it → 1',
        (det_dep(M, ['a', 'a', 'a'], {0, 1, 2}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=dmu, r_rho=drho + 1, u='p'),
         det_dep(M, ['a', 'a', 'a'], {0, 1, 2}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=dmu / 2, r_rho=drho + 1, u='p')), (0, 1))
    M = shape_model()     # z = 0: Δμ = 1, ρ = 0; z = 1: Δμ = 2, ρ = 1; both differences are 1
    add('Dep two thresholds (either side of the two differences): (r_μ, r_ρ) = (2, 0.5), (2, 1.5), (0.5, 1.5)',
        tuple(det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=a_, r_rho=b_, u='p') for a_, b_ in [(2, .5), (2, 1.5), (.5, 1.5)]), (1, 0, 1))
    add('Dep threshold domain: r_μ < 0 yields ⊥_Dom', det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=-1, u='p'), DOM)
    M = spontaneous_channel()     # act already differs before the probe, clause (a) fails, and the verdict comes from clause (b) alone
    add('Act_𝓟 clause (b) numeric increment threshold r_act set separately: spontaneous flow channel r_act = 0.5 yields 1, r_act = 5 yields 0',
        (det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=probe_class(M, 1)), det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=5, probes=probe_class(M, 1))), (1, 0))
    # the probe class 𝓟 is a frozen parameter: the adjudicator traverses only the declared probes and does not default to the whole alphabet (3.1(6), 4.3)
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    add('Act_𝓟 changes with the declared probe class (q=1, r_act=0.5, history w vs rest): 𝓟 = {rest} yields 0, 𝓟 = {p} yields 1, 𝓟 = all probes of length 1 yields 1',
        tuple(det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=ps) for ps in ([('rest',)], [('p',)], probe_class(M, 1))), (0, 1, 1))
    add('Prospective Act_𝓟 likewise changes with 𝓟: 𝓟 = {rest} yields 0, 𝓟 = {p} yields 1',
        (det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('rest',)]), det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)])), (0, 1))
    add('𝓟 undeclared yields ⊥_Undef; empty set yields 0; exceeding the length bound or alphabet yields ⊥_Dom; probe outside the set of legitimate histories yields ⊥_Dom',
        (det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5), det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[]),
         det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p', 'p')]), det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('x',)]),
         det_act(M, q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)], mon=(0, {(0, 'w'): 0, (0, 'rest'): 0}, {0}))),
        (UNDEF, 0, DOM, DOM, DOM))
    seen = set(); Mr = recording(spontaneous_channel(), seen)
    det_act_h(Mr, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('rest',)])
    add('With 𝓟 = {rest}, the only inputs actually applied during adjudication are w and rest from the history and the rest window; p outside 𝓟 is never applied', sorted(seen), ['rest', 'w'])
    # setting domain of Dep: a finite discrete level set must be fully enumerated; an infinite setting domain gives only in-range 0
    M = dep_unsampled()
    Vnat = ('infinite', lambda a: isinstance(a, int) and a >= 0)      # V_y = nonnegative integers (infinite), membership test executable
    add('Dep setting domain: V_y infinite, only {0,1,2} measured → in-range 0 with measured subdomain reported (no whole-domain 0); 3 included → 1; finite V_y disagreeing with sampling → ⊥_Dom; finite V_y={0,1,2} fully enumerated → 0',
        (det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=Vnat), det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2, 3], domain=Vnat),
         det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=[0, 1, 2, 3]), det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2])),
        ((IN0, (0, 1, 2)), 1, DOM, 0))
    add('Dep sampling legitimacy on an infinite domain: measured level −1 not in V_y → ⊥_Dom; writing only "infinite" without a membership test → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [-1, 0, 1], domain=Vnat), det_dep(M, ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain='infinite')), (DOM, DOM))
    # validity of the transition kernel: probabilities nonnegative and summing to 1; exact adjudication additionally requires a unique positive-probability successor
    bad = {'sum 0.8': lambda st, u: {st: 0.8}, 'negative probability': lambda st, u: {st: 1.2, (1 - st[0],): -0.2},
           'empty distribution': lambda st, u: {}, 'single point 0.3': lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else st): 0.3}}
    add('Invalid transition kernels (sum 0.8, negative probability, empty distribution, single-point probability 0.3): exact and enumeration both yield ⊥_Dom and do not pass as valid deterministic models',
        tuple((det_tr_exact(Model(['x'], [1], [1], f, (0,), ['w', 'rest']), q=1)[0],
               det_tr_enum(Model(['x'], [1], [1], f, (0,), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0]) for f in bad.values()),
        ((DOM, DOM),) * 4)
    add('Invalid disturbance distribution (sum 0.9): twin protocol yields ⊥_Dom',
        det_tr_enum(Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: (w,), {0: .5, 1: .4})), q=1, H=1)[0], DOM)
    add('Zero-probability entries do not change determinism: {successor: 1, other: 0} adjudicated as a deterministic transition',
        det_tr_exact(Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else st): 1.0, (7,): 0.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest']), q=1)[0], 1)
    # cross-beat disturbance: the ω_t of different beats are i.i.d. (shared by the two sides within a beat); cross-beat correlation must be declared as an augmented state (3.3)
    def iid_noise():          # x ← x ⊕ ω_t (on w beats only), ω_t a fair coin independent beat by beat
        return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: ((st[0] ^ w) if u == 'w' else st[0],), {0: .5, 1: .5}))
    def persistent_noise():   # same single-beat marginal, fully correlated across beats: the out-of-boundary augmented component c takes ω on the first beat and keeps it thereafter (2 means not yet taken); x ← x ⊕ c (on w beats only)
        def phi(st, u, w):
            x, c = st; cc = w if c == 2 else c
            return ((x ^ cc) if u == 'w' else x, cc)
        return Model(['x', 'c'], [1, 0], [1, 0], None, (0, 2), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(phi, {0: .5, 1: .5}))
    def tvx(M):
        a, b = run_twin(M, ['w', 'w', 'rest'], ['rest', 'w', 'rest'])
        return round(float(tv(push(a, lambda st: st[0]), push(b, lambda st: st[0]))), 4)
    add('Cross-beat independence and cross-beat correlation (same single-beat marginal) are not conflated: x-marginal TVs for the history pair (w,w) vs (rest,w) are 0 and 0.5',
        (tvx(iid_noise()), tvx(persistent_noise())), (0.0, 0.5))
    # structural representation and supply cut-off (3.3): a structural representation must declare cut_phi on the same disturbance space; giving only a cut-off kernel is an incomplete declaration, and cut readings yield ⊥_Undef
    def struct_cut(with_phi):
        phi = lambda st, u, w: ((1,) if u == 'w' else st)
        cut_k = lambda st: {(0,): 1.0}
        return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], cut=cut_k,
                     struct=(phi, {0: 1.0}, (lambda st, w: (0,))) if with_phi else (phi, {0: 1.0}))
    add('Structural representation + separate cut-off kernel (no cut_phi): Tr^cut, Hist^cut yield ⊥_Undef, Tr^rest as usual; with cut_phi added, Tr^cut has a valid value',
        (det_tr_enum(struct_cut(False), q=1, H=1, reading='cut')[0], det_hist(struct_cut(False), ['w'], {0}, q=1, reading='cut'),
         det_tr_enum(struct_cut(False), q=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(struct_cut(True), q=1, H=1, reading='cut')[0]),
        (UNDEF, UNDEF, 1, 0))
    # historical Act_𝓟^h and the set of legitimate histories (3.1(3)): ⊥_Dom when 𝓗 admits the original history but forbids the probe extension
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    MON_NO_P = (0, {(0, 'w'): 0, (0, 'rest'): 0}, {0})
    add('Act_𝓟^h: 𝓗 admits w, rest but forbids probe p → ⊥_Dom (no 1 from an illegitimate probe); with 𝓗 unconstrained the same 𝓟 yields 1; actual history not in 𝓗 → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)], mon=MON_NO_P),
         det_act_h(M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)]),
         det_act_h(M, ['p'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('rest',)], mon=MON_NO_P)), (DOM, 1, DOM))
    # single declaration entry (4.2): a family of erroneous declared parameters
    add('Declaration entry: q = −1, k = 0, H = 0, r_act < 0, malformed monitor automaton → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=-1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=1, k=0, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=1, H=0)[0],
         det_act(mirror(), q=1, H=1, P=1, r_act=-1, probes=[('rest',)]), det_tr_enum(mirror(), q=1, H=1, mon=('bad',))[0]),
        (DOM,) * 5)
    add('Declaration entry: empty position names, duplicate position name, initial-state length mismatch, alphabet missing rest → ⊥_Dom',
        (det_tr_enum(Model([], [], [], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(Model(['A', 'A'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(Model(['A'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (0, 0), ['w', 'rest']), q=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(Model(['A'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {st: 1.0}, (0,), ['w']), q=1, H=1)[0]),
        (DOM,) * 4)
    # 4.1 five-tuple report: every public adjudicator can be written as Det(v, Code, Level, Dom, Read)
    d1 = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1); d2 = report('Tr_exact', mutual_external(), q=4)
    d3 = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=Vnat); d4 = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1, reading='cut')
    d5 = report('Act_h', spontaneous_channel(), ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5); d6 = report('Hist', mirror(), ['w'], {0}, q=1)
    add('Five-tuple report: mirrored storage Tr (1, witness, Y₁); mutual write-back exact Tr (0, full-graph search); Dep infinite domain (0, in-range 0, measured subdomain)',
        ((d1.v, d1.Code, d1.Level, d1.Read['witness'] is not None), (d2.v, d2.Code.startswith('full-graph search'), d2.Read['shortest history length']),
         (d3.v, d3.Code.startswith('in-range 0'), d3.Read['measured subdomain'])), ((1, 'witness', 'Y₁', True), (0, True, None), (0, True, (0, 1, 2))))
    add('Five-tuple report: the ⊥ reason code goes into Code (mirrored storage with undeclared supply cut-off ⊥_Undef, 𝓟 undeclared ⊥_Undef); Hist yields 1; Dom contains adjudication mode, coupling protocol, 𝓗, start and C_op',
        ((d4.v, d4.Code), (d5.v, d5.Code), d6.v, sorted(d1.Dom), d1.Dom['C_op']['q']),
        (('⊥', UNDEF), ('⊥', UNDEF), 1, ['C_op', 'adjudication mode', 'coupling protocol', 'start s₀', '𝓗'], 1))
    # five-tuple Dom records the configuration actually in effect: positional and keyword arguments are equivalent; defaults are recorded; changing a parameter changes Dom
    ra = report('Tr', mirror(), 2, 0.05, 1, 2); rb = report('Tr', mirror(), q=2, r=0.05, k=1, H=2)
    rd = report('Tr', mirror()); rc = report('Tr', mirror(), q=2, r=0.05, k=1, H=3)
    add('Five-tuple Dom: positional and keyword arguments give the same Dom; a default call records the effective q=6, r=0.05, k=1, H=4; changing H changes Dom',
        (ra.Dom == rb.Dom, (rd.Dom['C_op']['q'], rd.Dom['C_op']['r'], rd.Dom['C_op']['k'], rd.Dom['C_op']['H']), rc.Dom != rb.Dom, rb.Dom['start s₀']),
        (True, (6, 0.05, 1, 4), True, (0, 0)))
    M = spontaneous_channel()
    pa = report('Act_h', M, ['w'], {0}, 1, 0.05, 1, probes=[('rest',)], r_act=0.5); pb = report('Act_h', M, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)])
    ma = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1, mon=MON_NO_W); mb = report('Tr', mirror(), q=1, H=1, mon=MON_W_AFTER_A)
    add('Five-tuple Dom: different probe sets give different Dom and the declared 𝓟 is recorded; the monitor automaton is recorded by a frozen digest, different 𝓗 giving different digests; the infinite setting domain of Dep records the membership test',
        (pa.Dom['C_op']['probes'], pb.Dom['C_op']['probes'], pa.Dom != pb.Dom, ma.Dom['𝓗'] != mb.Dom['𝓗'], ma.Dom['𝓗'].startswith('monitor automaton'),
         report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], {0}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=Vnat).Dom['C_op']['domain'][0]),
        ((('rest',),), (('p',),), True, True, True, 'infinite'))
    # probe legitimacy is checked before witnesses (3.1(3)(6)): with 𝓗 forbidding w, 𝓟 = {w} yields ⊥_Dom, not masked by the 0 of no witness
    add('Probe legitimacy first: 𝓗 forbids w, 𝓟 = {w}: prospective and historical forms both yield ⊥_Dom; legitimate empty 𝓟 yields 0; legitimate 𝓟 = {rest} with no witness yields 0',
        (det_act(mirror(), q=1, r=.05, k=1, H=2, P=1, probes=[('w',)], mon=MON_NO_W),
         det_act_h(mirror(), ['rest'], [0], q=1, P=1, probes=[('w',)], mon=MON_NO_W),
         det_act(mirror(), q=1, k=1, H=2, P=1, probes=[], mon=MON_NO_W),
         det_act(mirror(), q=1, k=1, H=2, P=1, probes=[('rest',)], mon=MON_NO_W)), (DOM, DOM, 0, 0))
    # history segment indices: type, bounds and duplicates must be valid
    add('History segment out of range, negative index, invalid type, duplicate index → ⊥_Dom (det_hist, det_act_h, det_dep); a legitimate segment is adjudicated as usual',
        (det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], [99], q=1), det_act_h(mirror(), ['w'], [99], q=1, probes=[]), det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [99], 0, 1, [0, 1]),
         det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], [-1], q=1), det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], ['0'], q=1), det_hist(mirror(), ['w', 'w'], [0, 0], q=1),
         det_hist(mirror(), ['w'], [0], q=1)), (DOM,) * 6 + (1,))
    # per-call override parameters: start dimension, variable index, input alphabet
    add('Start dimension mismatch, Dep variable index out of range, input not in alphabet → ⊥_Dom, no unhandled exception',
        (det_tr_enum(mirror(), init=(1,), q=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(mirror(), init=(1,), q=1)[0], det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 99, 1, [0, 1]),
         det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 99, [0, 1]), det_hist(mirror(), ['not_signal'], [0], q=1),
         det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], u='not_signal')), (DOM,) * 6)
    # frozen identity of membership tests on infinite setting domains
    def closure_member(v): return lambda a: v          # different closure values give different digests
    da = report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda x: True))
    db = report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda x: False))
    dc = report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda z: True))
    add('Membership-test identity: different implementations give different digests and Dom; repeated declaration of the same implementation gives the same Dom; a module global referenced without a declared identifier → ⊥_Dom, with a declared identifier recorded in Dom; different closure values give different digests',
        (da.Dom != db.Dom, da.Dom['C_op']['domain'] == report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', lambda x: True)).Dom['C_op']['domain'],
         da.Dom['C_op']['domain'] != dc.Dom['C_op']['domain'],
         det_dep(mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', _member_global)),
         report('Dep', mirror(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], domain=('infinite', _member_global, 'protocol G-1')).Dom['C_op']['domain'][1]['identifier'],
         _member_id(('infinite', closure_member(True)))[1] != _member_id(('infinite', closure_member(False)))[1]),
        (True, True, True, DOM, 'protocol G-1', True))
    # Lemma 2: an in-boundary copy that the in-boundary part itself maintains at rest yields 1; a copy cleared by rest does not meet the maintenance condition and yields 0
    clear = Model(['m'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == 'w' else (0,)): 1.0}, (0,), ['w', 'rest'])
    add('Lemma 2: the in-boundary copy of mirrored storage is maintained at rest, exact Tr = 1; a copy cleared by rest does not meet the maintenance condition, exact Tr = 0 (no conflict with Lemma 2)',
        (det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=1, k=1)[0], det_tr_exact(clear, q=1, k=1)[0]), (1, 0))
    # exact rational arithmetic: TV exactly equal to r yields 0 (strictly greater is required), r slightly below TV yields 1; the probability paths of Tr, Hist, Act, Dep and of the twin protocol are all exact
    def coin(pr): return Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: ({(0,): 1 - pr, (1,): pr} if u == 'w' and st == (0,) else {st: Fraction(1)}), (0,), ['w', 'rest'])
    def coin_s(pr): return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['w', 'rest'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: ((1,) if (u == 'w' and w == 1) else st), {0: 1 - pr, 1: pr}))
    third = Fraction(1, 3); eps_ = Fraction(1, 10**12)
    add('Exact threshold: when TV = r = 1/3, 1/7, Tr yields 0; when r = 1/3 − 10⁻¹², it yields 1; same under structural representation (twin protocol); same for Hist',
        (det_tr_enum(coin(third), q=0, r=third, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(coin(Fraction(1, 7)), q=0, r=Fraction(1, 7), k=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(coin(third), q=0, r=third - eps_, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(coin_s(third), q=0, r=third, k=1, H=1)[0],
         det_tr_enum(coin_s(third), q=0, r=third - eps_, k=1, H=1)[0], det_hist(coin(third), ['w'], [0], q=0, r=third),
         det_hist(coin(third), ['w'], [0], q=0, r=third - eps_)), (0, 0, 1, 0, 1, 0, 1))
    def coin_act(pr): return Model(['x', 'o'], [1, 0], [1, 1], lambda st, u: ({(0, 0): 1 - pr, (1, 0): pr} if (u == 'w' and st[0] == 0) else {(st[0], st[0] if u == 'p' else 0): Fraction(1)}),
                                   (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda st: st[1])
    add('Exact threshold: Act_𝓟 clause (a) (act TV after probe = 1/3) at r = 1/4 yields 1 and at r = 1/3 yields 0 (where Tr is also 0)',
        (det_act(coin_act(third), q=0, r=Fraction(1, 4), k=1, H=1, P=1, probes=[('p',)]), det_act(coin_act(third), q=0, r=third, k=1, H=1, P=1, probes=[('p',)])), (1, 0))
    # phased run of the cut reading: history → rest → supply cut-off → probe; rest-reading Act_h = 1, cut-reading Act_h = 0
    def wd(s_, u):
        y, o, g = s_
        return {(1 if u == 'w' else y, y * g if u == 'p' else 0, g): Fraction(1)}
    Aw = Model(['y', 'o', 'g'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], wd, (0, 0, 1), ['rest', 'w', 'p'], act=lambda s_: s_[1], cut=lambda s_: {(s_[0], 0, 0): Fraction(1)})
    seen_w = set(); Aw_r = recording(Aw, seen_w)
    add('Phased run of the cut reading: Hist^rest = Hist^cut = 1; Act_h^rest = 1, Act_h^cut = 0 (probe applied to the state after supply cut-off)',
        (det_hist(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, reading='rest'), det_hist(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, reading='cut'),
         det_act_h(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, reading='rest', probes=[('p',)]), det_act_h(Aw, ['w'], {0}, q=1, P=1, reading='cut', probes=[('p',)])), (1, 1, 1, 0))
    add('Phased runner: a cut-off step inside the sequence follows the cut-off law (after cut-off g = 0, so applying p then gives o = 0)',
        run_pair(Aw, ['w', 'rest', CUT, 'p'], ['rest', 'rest', CUT, 'p']), ({(1, 0, 0): 1}, {(0, 0, 0): 1}))
    # extended bound with a monitor automaton (Theorem 1 (c))
    MON20 = (0, {(i, u): i + 1 for i in range(20) for u in ('w', 'rest')}, {20})
    m20 = det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=MON20)
    add('Monitor-extended bound: 𝓗 accepting only length exactly 20 (21 states), shortest witness history length 20 ≤ search-derived bound 21²·B; expanded-node count is after the fact; without a monitor the bound has no factor',
        (m20[0], m20[1], m20[1] <= m20[4], m20[4] == 21 ** 2 * (m20[2] + m20[2] * m20[3] * 1), det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1)[4] == 6, m20[5] > 0), (1, 20, True, True, True, True))
    add('Monitor-extended bound: Tr = 0 with an empty accepting set; with a long legitimate prefix (5 rests required first) the shortest witness history length is 6',
        (det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=(0, {(0, 'w'): 0, (0, 'rest'): 0}, set()))[0],
         det_tr_exact(mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=(0, {**{(i, 'rest'): i + 1 for i in range(5)}, (5, 'w'): 5, (5, 'rest'): 5}, {5}))[1]), (0, 6))
    # self-contained witness for Lemma 7: redundant formation. a and b both write x to 1; the actual history (a, b) and the reference history (rest, b) end in the same state, Hist = 0; yet the actual a leaves an effective change 0 → 1 in its beat
    red = Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u in ('a', 'b') else st): Fraction(1)}, (0,), ['a', 'b', 'rest'])
    add('Lemma 7 (redundant formation): Hist((a, b) vs (rest, b)) = 0, while the first beat a of the actual history changes x from 0 to 1 (unlike the state after the first beat of the reference history)',
        (det_hist(red, ['a', 'b'], [0], q=1), run_pair(red, ['a'], ['rest'])), (0, ({(1,): 1}, {(0,): 1})))
    # the cut-off sentinel is kept apart from input names: a declared ordinary input '__cut' goes only to the transition law; a real supply cut-off goes through the cut-off law
    sig = lambda name: Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: {((1,) if u == name else st): Fraction(1)}, (0,), ['rest', name], cut=lambda st: {(0,): Fraction(1)})
    sig_s = lambda name: Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', name], struct=(lambda st, u, w: ((1,) if u == name else st), {0: 1}, lambda st, w: (0,)))
    add('Input name __cut does not clash with the cut-off sentinel: on both the transition-kernel and structural-representation paths, the __cut signal and the renamed write both yield Tr^rest = 1; under a real supply cut-off (reset to zero) Tr^cut = 0',
        tuple((det_tr_exact(f(nm), q=1, k=1)[0], det_tr_enum(f(nm), q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_enum(f(nm), q=1, k=1, H=1, reading='cut')[0])
              for f in (sig, sig_s) for nm in ('__cut', 'write')), ((1, 1, 0),) * 4)
    # the clause (b) write check under the cut reading uses all in-boundary components
    def ss(s_, u):
        y, o, z = s_
        if u == 'w': return {(1, 1, z): Fraction(1)}
        if u == 'p': return {(y, 2 * y, 1): Fraction(1)}
        return {s_: Fraction(1)}
    Mz = Model(['y', 'o', 'z'], [1, 0, 0], [1, 1, 1], ss, (0, 0, 0), ['rest', 'w', 'p'], act=lambda s_: s_[1], cut=lambda s_: {s_: Fraction(1)})
    add('Cut-reading clause (b) checks all in-boundary components: when p writes persistently into z outside the declared block, Act_h^cut = 0; spontaneous flow channel (p writes no in-boundary position, increment difference 2) Act_h^cut = 1',
        (det_act_h(Mz, ['w'], [0], q=1, reading='cut', P=1, probes=[('p',)], r=Fraction(1, 20), r_act=Fraction(1, 20)),
         det_act_h(spontaneous_channel(), ['w'], [0], q=1, reading='cut', P=1, r_act=0.5, probes=[('p',)])), (0, 1))
    # the membership-test digest includes keyword-only defaults
    def kw_factory(flag):
        def member(a, *, allowed=flag): return allowed
        return member
    kT = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=('infinite', kw_factory(True)))
    kF = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=('infinite', kw_factory(False)))
    kT2 = report('Dep', dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2], domain=('infinite', kw_factory(True)))
    add('Membership-test digest includes keyword-only defaults: allowed=True and False give different Dom (verdicts in-range 0 and ⊥_Dom); repeated declaration with the same frozen default gives the same Dom',
        (kT.Dom != kF.Dom, kT.v, kF.Code, kT.Dom == kT2.Dom), (True, 0, DOM, True))
    # tuple and list histories are equivalent
    rl, rt = report('Hist', mirror(), ['w'], [0], q=1), report('Hist', mirror(), ('w',), [0], q=1)
    add('Tuple and list actual histories are equivalent: Hist, Act_h, Dep give the same values, Hist reports the same Dom, no unhandled exception',
        (rl.v == rt.v == 1, rl.Dom == rt.Dom,
         det_act_h(mirror(), ('w',), [0], q=1, P=1, probes=[('rest',)]) == det_act_h(mirror(), ['w'], [0], q=1, P=1, probes=[('rest',)]),
         det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ('w',), [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2, 3]) == det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1, 2, 3]) == 1), (True, True, True, True))
    # search-derived bound and a priori bound kept separate
    rr_ = report('Tr_exact', mirror(), q=0, k=1, mon=MON20)
    add('Read field of exact adjudication: search-derived history-length upper bound 2646 and expanded nodes searched 100 kept separate; a priori history-length bound not provided (upper bounds on N and |S_in| must be declared)',
        (rr_.Read['search-derived upper bound on history length'], rr_.Read['expanded nodes searched'], rr_.Read['a priori history-length bound'].startswith('not provided')), (2646, 100, True))
    # Dep numeric readings are exact: float readings are converted to rationals at the boundary before subtraction, averaging, translation and comparison
    def dstep(st, u, one=1.0, tenth=.1):
        x, y, z = st
        return {(0, y, 1): 1} if u == 'w' else {((one if z else tenth) * y, y, z): 1}
    Mf = Model(['x', 'y', 'z'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], dstep, (0, 0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
    Mq = Model(['x', 'y', 'z'], [1, 1, 1], [1, 1, 1], lambda st, u: dstep(st, u, Fraction(1), Fraction(1, 10)), (0, 0, 0), ['w', 'rest'])
    tiny = Fraction(1, 10**12)
    add('Dep mean difference exactly 9/10: float-reading model at r_μ = 9/10 yields 0, at 9/10 − 10⁻¹² yields 1, at 9/10 + 10⁻¹² yields 0; identical case by case to the all-Fraction model',
        tuple((det_dep(m_, ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [0, 1], r_mu=rr, r_rho=1)) for m_ in (Mf, Mq) for rr in (Fraction(9, 10), Fraction(9, 10) - tiny, Fraction(9, 10) + tiny)),
        (0, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0))
    Mpre = Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: {((.3 if st[1] == 1 else .2), st[1]): 1}, (.2, 0), ['rest'])
    Mmix = Model(['x', 'y'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: ({(.3, 1): .5, (.2, 1): .5} if st[1] == 1 else {(.2, 0): 1.0}), (.2, 0), ['rest'])
    ep, em = y_effect(Mpre, (.2, 0), 0, 1, 0, 1), y_effect(Mmix, (.2, 0), 0, 1, 0, 1)
    add('Dep readings with nonzero pre-state and multi-point distribution: pre-state 0.2, post-state 0.3 give Δμ exactly 1/10 (float subtraction gives 0.09999…); multi-point distribution Δμ = 1/20, ρ = 1; all rational',
        (ep[0], em[0], em[1], all(isinstance(v, (int, Fraction)) for v in ep + em)), (Fraction(1, 10), Fraction(1, 20), 1, True))
    # float act readings are equally exact: clause (b) yields 0 when the increment difference equals r_act exactly
    def chan(table):
        def step(st, u):
            y, o = st
            if u == 'w': y = clip(y + 1, 0, 2)
            return {(y, 2 * y + 1 if u == 'p' else y): 1}
        return Model(['depth', 'flow'], [1, 0], [1, 1], step, (0, 0), ['w', 'p', 'rest'], act=lambda st: table[st[1]])
    fl, fr = {0: 0, 1: .1, 2: .2, 3: .3, 5: .5}, {k: Fraction(v).limit_denominator(10) for k, v in {0: 0, 1: .1, 2: .2, 3: .3, 5: .5}.items()}
    add('Float act readings (readings are decimal literals): when the increment difference equals r_act = 1/10 exactly, clause (b) yields 0, and with r_act slightly lower it yields 1; the 0.1 and Fraction(1, 10) reading models agree',
        tuple(det_act_h(chan(tb), ['w'], [0], q=1, P=1, r_act=ra, probes=[('p',)]) for tb in (fl, fr) for ra in (Fraction(1, 10), Fraction(1, 10) - tiny)),
        (0, 1, 0, 1))
    add('Reading policy: floating-point arithmetic in the model itself is interpreted by the values it produces — 0.1·3 produces 0.30000000000000004, taken as the exact value of its decimal representation, not rewritten as 3/10',
        (_qn(0.1 * 3) == Fraction('0.30000000000000004'), _qn(0.3) == Fraction(3, 10)), (True, True))
    # effective model representation: the start is normalized once at construction, the same start for every run entry; exogenous disturbances keep their declared keys, and every path uses the same validated distribution
    def a_model(cmp_val, init):
        return Model(['a', 'x'], [1, 1], [1, 1], lambda st, u: {(st[0], 1 if (u == 'w' and st[0] == cmp_val) else st[1]): 1}, init, ['rest', 'w'])
    rA = [(det_tr_enum(a_model(.1, ini), q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(a_model(.1, ini), q=1, k=1)[:2]) for ini in ((.1, 0), (Fraction(1, 10), 0))]
    rC = [(det_tr_enum(a_model(Fraction(1, 10), ini), q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(a_model(Fraction(1, 10), ini), q=1, k=1)[:2]) for ini in ((.1, 0), (Fraction(1, 10), 0))]
    expl = det_tr_exact(a_model(Fraction(1, 10), (0, 0)), q=1, k=1, init=(.1, 0))[:2]
    add('Same start representation: starts 0.1 and Fraction(1, 10) are both the rational 1/10; transitions are evaluated on rational states (comparison with float 0.1 is false: enumeration and exact both yield 0; comparison with 1/10: both yield 1, shortest history length 1); explicit and default starts agree',
        (rA, rC, expl), ([(0, (0, None))] * 2, [(1, (1, 1))] * 2, (1, 1)))
    Sd = Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, om: ((1,) if (u == 'w' and om == .1) else st), {.1: 1}))
    add('Exogenous disturbance keeps declared keys: direct call of φ, net transition (exact adjudication) and twin protocol (enumeration) all call φ with the declared value 0.1, yielding 1',
        (Sd.struct[0]((0,), 'w', .1), Sd.step((0,), 'w'), det_tr_exact(Sd, q=1, k=1)[:2], det_tr_enum(Sd, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0]), ((1,), {(1,): 1}, (1, 1), 1))
    Scut = Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, om: ((1,) if u == 'w' else st), {.1: 1}, lambda st, om: ((0,) if om == .1 else st)))
    add('Cut-off structure uses the same declared disturbance keys: cut_φ is called with 0.1, supply cut-off resets to zero, Tr^cut = 0, Tr^rest = 1 (enumeration and exact agree)',
        (det_tr_enum(Scut, q=1, k=1, H=1, reading='cut')[0], det_tr_exact(Scut, q=1, k=1, reading='cut')[0], det_tr_enum(Scut, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(Scut, q=1, k=1)[0]), (0, 0, 1, 1))
    # raw probabilities are validated entry by entry first, then merged or pushed forward
    Bk = Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: ({(.1,): Fraction(-1, 2), (Fraction(1, 10),): Fraction(3, 2)} if u == 'w' else {st: 1}), (0,), ['rest', 'w'])
    Ek = Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, om: st, {0: Fraction(-1, 2), 1: Fraction(3, 2)}))
    Pk = Model(['x'], [1], [1], lambda st, u: ({(.1,): Fraction(1, 2), (Fraction(1, 10),): Fraction(1, 2)} if u == 'w' else {st: 1}), (0,), ['rest', 'w'])
    add('Raw negative probabilities are not cancelled by merging or push-forward: state-key merging (enumeration, exact) and exogenous-disturbance push-forward (enumeration, exact) all yield ⊥_Dom; positive-probability successors 0.1 and 1/10 merge to probability 1 and yield 1 as usual',
        (det_tr_enum(Bk, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(Bk, q=1, k=1)[0], det_tr_enum(Ek, q=1, k=1, H=1)[0], det_tr_exact(Ek, q=1, k=1)[0],
         Pk.step((0,), 'w'), det_tr_exact(Pk, q=1, k=1)[0]), (DOM, DOM, DOM, DOM, {(Fraction(1, 10),): 1}, 1))
    # cross-check: on deterministic models, enumeration and exact adjudication agree in value and in shortest witness when the history length is covered
    def cross(M_, q_, k_):
        g = det_tr_exact(M_, q=q_, k=k_)
        H_ = g[1] if g[0] == 1 else 4
        e = det_tr_enum(M_, q=q_, k=k_, H=H_)
        return g[0] == e[0] and (g[0] != 1 or len(e[1][0]) == g[1])
    det_models = [(echo(), 6, 1), (conservative_pair(), 6, 1), (mirror(), 6, 1), (mutual_external(), 4, 1), (gate_once(), 1, 1), (long_prep(3), 1, 1),
                  (hybrid(3), 2, 1), (table_model(), 1, 2), (refresh_dram(), 2, 1), (spontaneous_channel(), 1, 1), (a_model(Fraction(1, 10), (.1, 0)), 1, 1), (Sd, 1, 1)]
    add(f'Cross-check: on {len(det_models)} deterministic models (including float starts and structural representations), enumeration and exact adjudication agree in value, and in shortest witness history length when the value is 1',
        all(cross(*m_) for m_ in det_models), True)
    add('Dep setting levels and a finite V_y compared in the same effective representation: declared V_y = {1/10, 1} with measured levels {0.1, 1} does not yield ⊥_Dom and gives the same value as the all-Fraction form',
        det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [.1, 1], domain=[Fraction(1, 10), 1]) == det_dep(dep_unsampled(), ['w'], [0], 0, 1, [Fraction(1, 10), 1], domain=[Fraction(1, 10), 1]) != DOM, True)
    # an invalid exogenous disturbance distribution detected at construction enters the single entry point: whether or not distinct legitimate history pairs exist, every public adjudication yields ⊥_Dom before evaluation
    MON_REST = (0, {(0, 'rest'): 0}, {0})
    bad_om = {'negative probability': {0: Fraction(-1, 2), 1: Fraction(3, 2)}, 'sum not 1': {0: Fraction(4, 5)}, 'empty distribution': {}, 'non-finite': {0: float('inf')}}
    def om_model(om): return Model(['x'], [1], [1], None, (0,), ['rest', 'w'], struct=(lambda st, u, w: st, om))
    res_om = []
    for om in bad_om.values():
        Mo = om_model(om)
        for mon_ in (MON_REST, None):
            res_om.append((validate_layer1_decl(Mo), det_tr_enum(Mo, q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=mon_)[0], det_tr_exact(Mo, q=1, k=1, mon=mon_)[0],
                           det_act(Mo, q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=mon_, probes=[]), det_hist(Mo, ['rest'], [0], q=1, mon=mon_), report('Tr', Mo, q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=mon_).v))
    add('Invalid exogenous disturbance distributions (negative probability, sum not 1, empty distribution, non-finite): with restricted legitimate histories (rest*, no distinct history pairs) and with the full sequence language, entry, Tr enumeration and exact, Act, Hist and report all yield ⊥_Dom; a valid distribution under rest* yields 0 as usual',
        (all(r_ == (DOM, DOM, DOM, DOM, DOM, '⊥') for r_ in res_om), det_tr_enum(om_model({0: 1}), q=1, k=1, H=2, mon=MON_REST)[0], det_tr_exact(om_model({0: 1}), q=1, k=1, mon=MON_REST)[0]),
        (True, 0, 0))
    add('Linear additive Dep', det_dep(linear_additive(), ['w', 'w'], {0, 1}, 0, 1, [0, 1, 2]), 0)
    add('Random terminal state Dep → ⊥_Obs', det_dep(prob_write(), ['w'], {0}, 0, 0, [0, 1]), OBS)
    # history retention under supply cut-off
    M = refresh_dram()
    add('Refresh-maintained charge Hist^cut/Hist^rest', (det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='cut', init=(0,)),
                                     det_hist(M, ['w'], {0}, q=2, reading='rest', init=(0,))), (0, 1))
    return T
if __name__ == '__main__':
    ok = n = 0
    for name, got, exp, mark in tests():
        print(f'{mark} {name:58s} verdict={got} expected={exp}'); ok += mark == '✓'; n += 1
    print(f'Total: {ok}/{n} items match')
~~~

A.2  layer1\_second.py  Second implementation of the first-layer Tr adjudication: brute-force enumeration and pair automata (Theorems 1 and 2, Lemma 2)

~~~python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field, Appendix A.2: second implementation of the first-layer Tr adjudication (brute-force enumeration and the pair automaton, two methods that cross-check each other; written independently of Appendix A.1).
Witness models: M1 (where the N²(k+1) bound of Theorem 1 (a2) fails), M2 (Theorem 1 (b)), M3 (Theorem 2 (b)), M4 (Lemma 2).
Model: T(x,u) -> x'; states are tuples; inside = indices of the in-boundary components, outside = indices of the out-of-boundary components.
Clamping: at each step, after both sides have made their own transitions, the out-of-boundary components of the h'-side state are replaced by those of the h side at the same step.
Witness: h != h', number of differing positions <= k, history length <= H, readings differ after q (clamped) rest steps.
The reading read(x) defaults to the in-boundary components (reading mode “rest”).
"""
import itertools
from collections import deque

class Model:
    def __init__(self, name, s0, alphabet, T, inside, outside, read=None):
        self.name, self.s0, self.alphabet, self.T = name, s0, alphabet, T
        self.inside, self.outside = inside, outside
        self.read = read or (lambda x: tuple(x[i] for i in inside))
    def clamp(self, sp, s):
        if not self.outside:
            return sp
        y = list(sp)
        for i in self.outside:
            y[i] = s[i]
        return tuple(y)
    def step(self, s, sp, u, up, clamped):
        s1 = self.T(s, u)
        sp1 = self.T(sp, up)
        if clamped:
            sp1 = self.clamp(sp1, s1)
        return s1, sp1
    def rest_differs(self, s, sp, q, clamped):
        for _ in range(q):
            s, sp = self.step(s, sp, 'rest', 'rest', clamped)
        return self.read(s) != self.read(sp)
    def reachable(self):
        seen = {self.s0}
        dq = deque([self.s0])
        while dq:
            x = dq.popleft()
            for u in self.alphabet:
                y = self.T(x, u)
                if y not in seen:
                    seen.add(y)
                    dq.append(y)
        return seen

# ---------------- Method 1: brute-force enumeration ----------------
def run_pair(M, h, hp, q, clamped):
    s = sp = M.s0
    for u, up in zip(h, hp):
        s, sp = M.step(s, sp, u, up, clamped)
    return M.rest_differs(s, sp, q, clamped)

def brute_explicit(M, q, k, H, clamped=True):
    """Explicit pair-by-pair enumeration (no prefix sharing of any kind); returns the shortest witness (length, h, h') or None."""
    A = M.alphabet
    for L in range(1, H + 1):
        for h in itertools.product(A, repeat=L):
            for d in range(1, k + 1):
                for pos in itertools.combinations(range(L), d):
                    choices = [[a for a in A if a != h[p]] for p in pos]
                    for sub in itertools.product(*choices):
                        hp = list(h)
                        for p, a in zip(pos, sub):
                            hp[p] = a
                        if run_pair(M, h, tuple(hp), q, clamped):
                            return L, h, tuple(hp)
    return None

def brute_tree(M, q, k, H, clamped=True):
    """Exhaustively enumerate all history pairs (DFS shares prefix computation but merges no states, so it still enumerates history pair by history pair).
    Returns the shortest history length L at which a witness exists, and one witness."""
    A = M.alphabet
    best = [H + 1, None]
    count = [0]
    path = []
    def dfs(s, sp, j, d):
        for u in A:
            for up in A:
                jj = j + (u != up)
                if jj > k:
                    continue
                s1, sp1 = M.step(s, sp, u, up, clamped)
                count[0] += 1
                path.append((u, up))
                if jj >= 1 and d + 1 < best[0] and M.rest_differs(s1, sp1, q, clamped):
                    best[0], best[1] = d + 1, list(path)
                if d + 1 < min(H, best[0] - 1):
                    dfs(s1, sp1, jj, d + 1)
                path.pop()
    dfs(M.s0, M.s0, 0, 0)
    if best[1] is None:
        return None, None, None, count[0]
    h = tuple(a for a, _ in best[1])
    hp = tuple(b for _, b in best[1])
    return best[0], h, hp, count[0]

# ---------------- Method 2: pair-automaton BFS ----------------
def bfs(M, q, k, clamped=True):
    start = (M.s0, M.s0, 0)
    parent = {start: None}
    depth = {start: 0}
    dq = deque([start])
    accept = None
    A = M.alphabet
    while dq:
        node = dq.popleft()
        s, sp, j = node
        if j >= 1 and accept is None and M.rest_differs(s, sp, q, clamped):
            accept = node
            # no early termination: search the full graph to count the nodes and |I'|
        for u in A:
            for up in A:
                jj = j + (u != up)
                if jj > k:
                    continue
                s1, sp1 = M.step(s, sp, u, up, clamped)
                nn = (s1, sp1, jj)
                if nn not in parent:
                    parent[nn] = (node, u, up)
                    depth[nn] = depth[node] + 1
                    dq.append(nn)
    # BFS dequeues in depth order, so the first accepting node found has minimal depth
    Iprime = {tuple(sp[i] for i in M.inside) for (s, sp, j) in parent if j >= 1}
    res = dict(nodes=len(parent), Iprime=len(Iprime), Tr=0, L=None, h=None, hp=None)
    if accept is not None:
        h, hp = [], []
        n = accept
        while parent[n] is not None:
            p, u, up = parent[n]
            h.append(u); hp.append(up); n = p
        res.update(Tr=1, L=depth[accept], h=tuple(reversed(h)), hp=tuple(reversed(hp)))
    return res

# ---------------- Models ----------------
def T_M1(x, u):
    m, E = x
    if u == 'w':
        return (1, 1)
    if m != E:
        if u == 'a':
            return (min(12, m + 1), E)
        if u == 'rest' and m < 12:
            return (E, E)
        return (m, E)
    return (m, E)

M1 = Model('M1 clamped mixed state', (0, 0), ['w', 'a', 'rest'], T_M1, [0], [1])

def make_M2(n=6, read_x_only=False):
    def T(x, u):
        c, xx = x
        if u == 'a':
            return (min(n, c + 1), xx)
        if u == 'w' and c >= n:
            return (c, 1)
        return (0, xx)
    read = (lambda s: (s[1],)) if read_x_only else None
    return Model('M2 long preparatory history n=%d%s' % (n, ' compare x only' if read_x_only else ' rest'),
                 (0, 0), ['a', 'w', 'rest'], T, [0, 1], [], read)

M3_TAB = {
    (0, 0): {'a': (0, 0), 'b': (1, 0), 'rest': (0, 0)},
    (0, 1): {'a': (0, 1), 'b': (0, 0), 'rest': (0, 0)},
    (1, 0): {'a': (0, 1), 'b': (0, 0), 'rest': (0, 1)},
    (1, 1): {'a': (1, 0), 'b': (0, 1), 'rest': (1, 1)},
}
M3 = Model('M3 TAB counterexample', (0, 0), ['a', 'b', 'rest'], lambda x, u: M3_TAB[x][u], [0], [1])

def T_M4(x, u):
    m, E = x
    m2, E2 = E, m
    if u == 'w':
        m2 = 1
    return (m2, E2)

M4 = Model('M4 mutual write-back to external store', (0, 0), ['w', 'rest'], T_M4, [0], [1])

# ---------------- Main program ----------------
def fmt(r):
    s = 'Tr=%d nodes=%d |I\'|=%d' % (r['Tr'], r['nodes'], r['Iprime'])
    if r['Tr']:
~~~
