~~~python
        s += ' shortest witness history length=%d h=%s h\'=%s' % (r['L'], ','.join(r['h']), ','.join(r['hp']))
    return s

def trace(M, h, hp, q, clamped=True):
    s = sp = M.s0
    lines = ['  init h:%s h\':%s' % (s, sp)]
    for u, up in list(zip(h, hp)) + [('rest', 'rest')] * q:
        s1 = M.T(s, u); raw = M.T(sp, up)
        sp1 = M.clamp(raw, s1) if clamped else raw
        lines.append('  %s/%s -> h:%s  h\':%s(unclamped)->%s' % (u, up, s1, raw, sp1))
        s, sp = s1, sp1
    lines.append('  readings h=%s h\'=%s differ=%s' % (M.read(s), M.read(sp), M.read(s) != M.read(sp)))
    return '\n'.join(lines)

def main():
    out = []
    P = out.append
    # ---- M1 ----
    P('=== M1 clamped mixed state, q=2, k=1 ===')
    N = len(M1.reachable())
    P('reachable states N=%d  %s' % (N, sorted(M1.reachable())))
    r = bfs(M1, 2, 1, True)
    P('BFS clamped: ' + fmt(r))
    P('N^2(k+1)=%d  node count %s this value' % (N * N * 2, 'exceeds' if r['nodes'] > N * N * 2 else 'does not exceed'))
    P('N + N*|I\'|*k = %d  (>= shortest witness %s ? %s)' % (N + N * r['Iprime'] * 1, r['L'],
                                                  N + N * r['Iprime'] >= (r['L'] or 0)))
    P(trace(M1, r['h'], r['hp'], 2))
    P('BFS unclamped (reference): ' + fmt(bfs(M1, 2, 1, False)))
    for H in (11, 12, 13):
        L, h, hp, cnt = brute_tree(M1, 2, 1, H, True)
        P('brute force (tree enumeration) H=%d: shortest witness=%s  %s  (history-pair prefixes enumerated %d)' % (
            H, L, ('h=%s h\'=%s' % (','.join(h), ','.join(hp))) if L else '', cnt))
    P('brute force (explicit pairwise) H=9: %s' % (brute_explicit(M1, 2, 1, 9, True),))
    # ---- M2 ----
    for xo in (False, True):
        M2 = make_M2(6, xo)
        P('\n=== %s, q=1, k=1 ===' % M2.name)
        P('reachable states N=%d' % len(M2.reachable()))
        r = bfs(M2, 1, 1, True)
        P('BFS: ' + fmt(r))
        P(trace(M2, r['h'], r['hp'], 1))
        P('brute force (explicit pairwise) H=6: %s' % (brute_explicit(M2, 1, 1, 6, True),))
        P('brute force (explicit pairwise) H=7: %s' % (brute_explicit(M2, 1, 1, 7, True),))
    # ---- M3 ----
    P('\n=== M3 TAB counterexample, q=1 ===')
    P('reachable states N=%d  %s' % (len(M3.reachable()), sorted(M3.reachable())))
    for clamped in (True, False):
        for k in (1, 2):
            r = bfs(M3, 1, k, clamped)
            P('BFS %s k=%d: %s' % ('clamped' if clamped else 'unclamped', k, fmt(r)))
            L, h, hp, cnt = brute_tree(M3, 1, k, 8, clamped)
            P('   brute force (tree enumeration) H=8: shortest witness=%s %s' % (
                L, ('h=%s h\'=%s' % (','.join(h), ','.join(hp))) if L else ''))
            P('   brute force (explicit pairwise) H=6: %s' % (brute_explicit(M3, 1, k, 6, clamped),))
    for h, hp in ((('b', 'a'), ('a', 'b')), (('a', 'b'), ('b', 'a'))):
        P('history pair h=%s h\'=%s clamped q=1 is a witness: %s' % (h, hp, run_pair(M3, h, hp, 1, True)))
        P(trace(M3, h, hp, 1))
        P('  same history pair unclamped: %s' % run_pair(M3, h, hp, 1, False))
    # ---- M4 ----
    P('\n=== M4 mutual write-back to external store, q=4, k=1 ===')
    P('reachable states N=%d  %s' % (len(M4.reachable()), sorted(M4.reachable())))
    for clamped in (False, True):
        r = bfs(M4, 4, 1, clamped)
        P('BFS %s: %s' % ('clamped' if clamped else 'unclamped', fmt(r)))
        if r['Tr']:
            P(trace(M4, r['h'], r['hp'], 4, clamped))
        P('   brute force (explicit pairwise) H=10: %s' % (brute_explicit(M4, 4, 1, 10, clamped),))
    print('\n'.join(out))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.3  t3cd.py  Exact distribution computation for Theorem 2 (c)(d)

~~~python
"""The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field, Appendix A.3: exact distribution computation for Theorem 2 (c)(d) (no sampling)."""
import itertools
from collections import defaultdict
def tv(a,b): ks=set(a)|set(b); return .5*sum(abs(a.get(k,0)-b.get(k,0)) for k in ks)
def run(step, s0, h):
    d={s0:1.0}
    for u in h:
        n=defaultdict(float)
        for s,p in d.items():
            for t,q in step(s,u).items(): n[t]+=p*q
        d=dict(n)
    return d
# (c) random write: x∈{0,1}; w flips 0→1 with probability ε
eps=0.05
st=lambda s,u: ({(1,):eps,(0,):1-eps} if (u=='w' and s[0]==0) else {s:1.0})
for j in (1,2,3):
    d1=run(st,(0,),['w']*j+['rest']); d2=run(st,(0,),['rest']*j+['rest'])
    print(f'(c) j={j} differing positions: TV={tv(d1,d2):.4f}  analytic 1-(1-ε)^j={1-(1-eps)**j:.4f}')
# full search: maximum TV over history pairs differing at no more than k positions, history length ≤ 4
def maxTV(step,s0,alpha,k,H,q=1):
    best=0
    for L in range(1,H+1):
        for h in itertools.product(alpha,repeat=L):
            for kk in range(1,min(k,L)+1):
                for pos in itertools.combinations(range(L),kk):
                    for alt in itertools.product(alpha,repeat=kk):
                        h2=list(h)
                        for p,a in zip(pos,alt): h2[p]=a
                        if tuple(h2)==h: continue
                        best=max(best,tv(run(step,s0,list(h)+['rest']*q),run(step,s0,h2+['rest']*q)))
    return best
print('(c) r=0.08: k=1 max TV (H≤4)=%.4f; k=2 max TV=%.4f' % (maxTV(st,(0,),['w','rest'],1,4), maxTV(st,(0,),['w','rest'],2,4)))
# (d) gated clock: c∈{0..3} advances by 1 mod 4 each step; at c=0, w increments x with probability 0.04 (capped at x=3)
G,e=4,0.04
def gst(s,u):
    c,x=s; c2=(c+1)%G
    if u=='w' and c==0: return {(c2,min(3,x+1)):e,(c2,x):1-e}
    return {(c2,x):1.0}
r=0.05
def contig_max(Lseg,H):
    best=0
    for L in range(Lseg,H+1):
        for start in range(L-Lseg+1):
            for seg in itertools.product(['w','rest'],repeat=Lseg):
                h=['rest']*L; h2=list(h)
                for i,a in enumerate(seg): h2[start+i]=a
                if h2==h: continue
                best=max(best,tv(run(gst,(0,0),h+['rest']),run(gst,(0,0),h2+['rest'])))
    return best
print('(d) positional reading: k=2 max TV (H≤9)=%.4f' % maxTV(gst,(0,0),['w','rest'],2,9))
for Lseg in (1,2,3,4,5):
    print(f'(d) contiguous-segment reading: segments of length {Lseg} (baseline all rest, H≤9) max TV = {contig_max(Lseg,9):.4f}')
~~~

A.4  mtm.py  Mechanism transition model, mechanism-level declaration entry (consistent with the upstream F domain), three observation levels, perturbation coverage, declaration and negative-determination antecedent list, mechanism-level identification, ⊥ reason codes, five-tuple report adapter (same source as the atomic reports) and the in-boundary self-feedback-type verdict (3.5, 4.6, 4.8, Theorem 6, Lemma 6.1)

~~~python
"""The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field, Appendix A.4: the mechanism transition model (MTM), three observation levels and the mechanism-level identification program.
MTM is a mechanism model with input: at each beat the input u determines the out-of-boundary pulses and scales the numeric law of each mechanism by a gating factor.
Once an input history is fixed, each mechanism at each beat is a separable mechanism of the F layer of The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization, so the three duties of the actual history
are computed directly by the evaluator field_final.run of The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A.1 (ground truth).
Three kinds of observation:
  Y1  profile level: net transition N(t, s, u) (any state, any input) and activity read-out act(s);
  Y2  mechanism level: per-mechanism knockout KO, local setting LOC at Trace-Response positions, read-out Out;
  Ydecl  declaration: per-beat mechanism inventory (name, carrier, object, whether it runs) and inventory completeness INV-c.
"""
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import os as _os
sys.path.insert(0, _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # field_final.py sits in the same directory as this file (identical, character for character, to The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A.1)
from field_final import Spec, Pulse, Process, Link, run, exact
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Mech:
    name: str
    target: str
    inside: bool                 # carrier inside B
    law: str                     # constant / linear / bilinear
    reads: tuple = ()
    k: Fr = Fr(0)
    b: Fr = Fr(0)
    gate: tuple = ()             # ((u, factor), ...); inputs not listed have factor 1; factor 0 means the mechanism does not run at that beat
    beats: tuple | None = None   # None means it may run at every beat
    def factor(self, u):
        for uu, f in self.gate:
            if uu == u:
                return Fr(f)
        return Fr(1)
    def runs(self, t, u):
        return (self.beats is None or t in self.beats) and self.factor(u) != 0
    def value(self, s, u):
        g = self.factor(u); k = self.k * g; b = self.b * g
        if self.law == 'constant':
            return b
        if self.law == 'linear':
            return b + k * sum(s[r] for r in self.reads)
        return b + k * s[self.reads[0]] * s[self.reads[1]]

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MTM:
    sites: tuple
    s0: tuple
    T: tuple
    eps: Fr
    t0: int
    t1: int
    pulses: tuple = ()           # ((u, ((target, amount), ...)), ...), all out-of-boundary signal pulses
    mechs: tuple = ()
    def pulses_of(self, u):
        for uu, ps in self.pulses:
            if uu == u:
                return ps
        return ()
    def beats(self):
        return list(range(self.t0 - 1, self.t1 + 1))

# ---------- Ground truth: actual history → Mathematical Form F specification → field_final ----------
def to_spec(M, hist, T=None):
    """F specification of the reference truth, generated from the known model structure: the read positions are Mech.reads, read certification is set to 1, and one evidence edge per read position is set to 1.
    It is "the F encoding of the known model", used to check whether Y₂ inversion reproduces that encoding; it is not a read and route certification obtained by independent material observation."""
    assert len(hist) == len(M.beats())
    pulses = []; procs = []; links = []
    for t, u in zip(M.beats(), hist):
        for j, (tg, a) in enumerate(M.pulses_of(u)):
            pulses.append(Pulse(f'{u}@{t}#{j}', t, tg, a, True, True))
        for m in M.mechs:
            if not m.runs(t, u):
                continue
            g = m.factor(u)
            nm = f'{m.name}@{t}'
            procs.append(Process(nm, t, m.target, m.inside, m.law, tuple(m.reads), m.k * g, m.b * g, True, True))
            if t >= M.t0:
                for r in m.reads:
                    links.append(Link(f'state:{r}:{t}', nm, True))
    return Spec(tuple(M.sites), tuple(M.s0), tuple(range(M.t0, M.t1 + 1)), tuple(pulses), tuple(procs),
                tuple(links), True, True, True, M.eps, tuple(T if T is not None else M.T))

def truth(M, hist, T=None):
    r = run(to_spec(M, hist, T))
    return {k: r[k] for k in ('trace', 'part', 'hdyn', 'Q')}

# ---------- Y1: profile-level observation ----------
def net(M, t, s, u, drop=None):
    """Net transition; drop is the name of a knocked-out mechanism or a pulse name 'pulse#j' (for Y2 only). Mathematical Form 4.2: only effective components are added."""
    eps = M.eps; new = dict(s)
    for j, (tg, a) in enumerate(M.pulses_of(u)):
        a = exact(a)
        if a > eps and drop != f'pulse#{j}':
            new[tg] += a
    for m in M.mechs:
        if m.name == drop or not m.runs(t, u):
            continue
        c = m.value(s, u)
        if abs(c) > eps:
            new[m.target] += c
    return new

def trajectory(M, hist):
    s = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(M.sites, M.s0)}
    out = [dict(s)]
    for t, u in zip(M.beats(), hist):
        s = net(M, t, s, u)
        out.append(dict(s))
    return out

def poly_of(M, t, u):
    """With ε = 0 the net increment is a polynomial in the state; returns a dictionary of monomials (for exact verification of profile equality)."""
    P = {}
    def add(key, v):
        P[key] = P.get(key, Fr(0)) + v
    for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u):
        add((tg, ()), exact(a))
    for m in M.mechs:
        if not m.runs(t, u):
            continue
        g = m.factor(u)
        add((m.target, ()), m.b * g)
        if m.law == 'linear':
            for r in m.reads:
                add((m.target, (r,)), m.k * g)
        elif m.law == 'bilinear':
            add((m.target, tuple(sorted(m.reads))), m.k * g)
    return {k: v for k, v in P.items() if v != 0}

def same_profile(M1, M2, alphabet):
    """Profile equality: same position set, same initial state, same window, and the net-increment polynomials identical at every beat for every input (requires ε = 0)."""
    assert M1.eps == 0 and M2.eps == 0
    if M1.sites != M2.sites or M1.s0 != M2.s0 or (M1.t0, M1.t1) != (M2.t0, M2.t1):
        return False
    return all(poly_of(M1, t, u) == poly_of(M2, t, u) for t in M1.beats() for u in alphabet)

def hist_W(M, hist, seg, rest='rest'):
    """Window-end reading Hist: compare all in-boundary states at t1+1 between the actual history and the reference history with segment seg replaced by rest (deterministic, TV ∈ {0,1})."""
    ref = [rest if i in seg else u for i, u in enumerate(hist)]
    return trajectory(M, hist)[-1] != trajectory(M, ref)[-1]

def dep0(M, t, s, u, x, y, a, b):
    """Basic dependence effect: whether the one-step net change of x differs between setting y = a and y = b."""
    sa = dict(s); sa[y] = exact(a)
    sb = dict(s); sb[y] = exact(b)
    return (net(M, t, sa, u)[x] - sa[x]) != (net(M, t, sb, u)[x] - sb[x])

# ---------- Y2: mechanism-level observation and the identification program ----------
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decl:
    """Declarations and their certification status. Everything defaults to undeclared: an antecedent without certification is not taken to be true by default.
    proto: SEP, MOD, LOC, BEAT, INV-a (the atomic adjudication protocols of 4.6); inv_c, rec, ent, pos, ef4: see 4.2 and Theorem 7; outf: names of the processes declared OUT-f."""
    proto: bool = False
    inv_c: bool = False
    rec: bool = False
    ent: bool = False
    pos: bool = False
    ef4: bool = False
    outf: frozenset = frozenset()

def full_decl(M, **kw):
    """All antecedents declared (linear-law processes declared OUT-f); kw can withdraw any of them individually."""
    d = dict(proto=True, inv_c=True, rec=True, ent=True, pos=True, ef4=True,
             outf=frozenset(m.name for m in M.mechs if m.law == 'linear'))
    d.update(kw)
    return Decl(**d)

def strip(decl, key):
    """Withdraw one antecedent: PROTO, INV-c, REC, ENT, POS, EF4, OUT-f."""
    m = {'PROTO': 'proto', 'INV-c': 'inv_c', 'REC': 'rec', 'ENT': 'ent', 'POS': 'pos', 'EF4': 'ef4'}
    if key == 'OUT-f':
        return Decl(**{**decl.__dict__, 'outf': frozenset()})
    return Decl(**{**decl.__dict__, m[key]: False})

@dataclass
class Lab:
    """Host test bench. With modular=False a knockout triggers compensation (violates MOD); with side=(i, j) setting i also changes j (violates LOC);
    mechanisms in hidden do not appear in the declared inventory (violates INV-c)."""
    M: MTM
    modular: bool = True
    compensate: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # when m is knocked out, mechanism m2 restores m's component: {m: m2}
    side: tuple | None = None
    hidden: tuple = ()
    out_mode: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # process name → 'faithful' (OUT-f: faithful read-out) / 'const' (output decoupled from reading); default: faithful for linear laws, no read-out otherwise
    pert: dict = field(default_factory=dict)       # position → legitimate perturbation δ (default +1), 'none' (no legitimate nonzero perturbation) or 'unapplied' (exists but not applied)
    def inventory(self):
        return [(m.name, m.inside, m.target) for m in self.M.mechs if m.name not in self.hidden]
    def set_local(self, s, i, v):
        s2 = dict(s); s2[i] = exact(v)
        if self.side and self.side[0] == i:
            s2[self.side[1]] = s2[self.side[1]] + 1
        return s2
    def ko(self, t, s, u, name):
        new = net(self.M, t, s, u, drop=name)
        if not self.modular and name in self.compensate:
            m = next(mm for mm in self.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
            if m.runs(t, u):
                c = m.value(s, u)
                if abs(c) > self.M.eps:
                    new[m.target] += c         # the compensating mechanism restores exactly this amount; the knockout difference is zero
        return new
    def full(self, t, s, u):
        return net(self.M, t, s, u)
    def delta(self, i, s):
        """Legitimate perturbation of position i: returns δ, 'none' or 'unapplied'."""
        d = self.pert.get(i, Fr(1))
        return d
    def out(self, t, s, u, name):
        """Process read-out. OUT-f (faithful): the read-out is the tuple of values at the positions the process actually reads (a constant-law process actually reads no position, so its read-out is the empty tuple);
        const: the read-out is a fixed constant, decoupled from reading; None: no read-out. The default agrees with Mathematical Form D15: faithful for linear laws, no read-out otherwise."""
        m = next(mm for mm in self.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
        if not m.runs(t, u):
            return None
        mode = self.out_mode.get(name, 'faithful' if m.law == 'linear' else None)
        if mode == 'faithful':
            return tuple(s[r] for r in m.reads)
        if mode == 'const':
            return ('c',)
        return None
    def faithful(self, name):
        m = next(mm for mm in self.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
        return self.out_mode.get(name, 'faithful' if m.law == 'linear' else None) == 'faithful'

# ---------- Three-valued verdicts and negative-determination bases ----------
# A verdict value is (v, B): v ∈ {True, False, None}; when v is False, B is the set of antecedents that this 0 consumes.
# A conjunction that is 0 takes the intersection of the bases of its 0 conjuncts (withdrawing any one of them invalidates every 0 path); a disjunction that is 0 takes the union.
# When v is None, the second component is the set of reason codes for ⊥ (⊥_Prof, ⊥_Pert, ⊥_Obs, ⊥_Sub, ⊥_Inv, ⊥_Rec, ⊥_Id).
V1 = (True, frozenset()); VU = (None, frozenset())
def V0(*b): return (False, frozenset(b))
def U(*codes): return (None, frozenset(codes))
def AND(*xs):
    zs = [b for v, b in xs if v is False]
    if zs: return (False, frozenset.intersection(*zs))
    if any(v is None for v, _ in xs): return (None, frozenset().union(*[b for v, b in xs if v is None]))
    return V1
def OR(*xs):
    if any(v is True for v, _ in xs): return V1
    if any(v is None for v, _ in xs): return (None, frozenset().union(*[b for v, b in xs if v is None]))
    return (False, frozenset().union(*[b for _, b in xs]))
def _tor(xs):
    xs = list(xs)
    return True if True in xs else (None if None in xs else False)
def _tand(*xs):
    if False in xs: return False
    if None in xs: return None
    return True

DOM = '⊥_Dom'
_LAWS = {'constant': 0, 'linear': 1, 'bilinear': 2}
def validate_mechanism_decl(lab, hist, decl, T=None):
    """Single entry point for mechanism-level declarations (Mathematical Form D20; this Form 3.5, 4.2): returns None (passed) or (DOM, reason).
    An invalid declaration does not enter evaluation; a certification that was not submitted is not reported as an error here, but yields ⊥ in the verdict through the missing item in decl."""
    M = lab.M
    def bad(why): return (DOM, why)
    num = lambda v: isinstance(v, (int, Fr)) and not isinstance(v, bool)
    if not M.sites or len(set(M.sites)) != len(M.sites) or not all(isinstance(x, str) and x for x in M.sites): return bad('position name empty or duplicated')
    if len(M.s0) != len(M.sites) or not all(num(v) and v >= 0 for v in M.s0): return bad('start state does not match the position set or contains a negative value')
    if not (num(M.eps) and M.eps >= 0): return bad('ε is not a nonnegative rational')
    if not (isinstance(M.t0, int) and isinstance(M.t1, int) and 1 <= M.t0 <= M.t1): return bad('declared period t₀, t₁ is invalid')
    TT = tuple(T if T is not None else M.T)
    if not TT or len(set(TT)) != len(TT) or not set(TT) <= set(M.sites): return bad('declared T is empty, duplicated or outside the position set')
    names = [m.name for m in M.mechs]
    if len(set(names)) != len(names): return bad('duplicate process name')
    win = set(M.beats())
    for m in M.mechs:
        if m.target not in M.sites: return bad(f'the action target of process {m.name} is missing or outside the position set')
        if m.law not in _LAWS: return bad(f'the law of process {m.name} is not F-class')
        if not set(m.reads) <= set(M.sites): return bad(f'the read positions of process {m.name} are outside the position set')
        if m.law == 'bilinear' and len(m.reads) != 2: return bad(f'the bilinear law of process {m.name} must read exactly two positions')
        if m.law == 'linear' and not m.reads: return bad(f'the linear law of process {m.name} has no read positions')
        if m.law == 'constant' and m.reads: return bad(f'the constant law of process {m.name} must not have read positions')
        if not (num(m.k) and num(m.b)): return bad(f'the parameters of process {m.name} are not rational')
        if m.beats is not None and not set(m.beats) <= win: return bad(f'the run beats of process {m.name} fall outside the declared window')
    for u, ps in M.pulses:
        for tg, a in ps:
            if tg not in M.sites or not (num(a) and a >= 0): return bad(f'the pulse object or amplitude of input {u} is invalid')
    if len(hist) != len(M.beats()): return bad('input history length does not match the number of beats')
    for i, d in lab.pert.items():
        if i not in M.sites: return bad(f'perturbation position {i} is outside the position set')
        if not (d in ('none', 'unapplied') or (num(d) and d != 0)): return bad(f'the perturbation of position {i} is neither a valid nonzero value nor a declared status')
    if not isinstance(decl, Decl) or not set(decl.outf) <= set(names): return bad('invalid declaration format, or OUT-f names a nonexistent process')
    # The actual trajectory is generated exactly, beat by beat; any negative position leaves the F domain (Mathematical Form 4.2, D20; no truncation at zero, no tolerance), and evaluation stops
    st = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(M.sites, M.s0)}
    for t, u in zip(M.beats(), hist):
        st = net(M, t, st, u)
        for x in M.sites:
            if st[x] < 0: return bad(f'after beat {t}, position {x} has depth {st[x]}: negative value outside the F domain')
    return None
def identify(lab, hist, decl, T=None, detail=False):
    """Build the Mathematical Form F atoms (three-valued, with negative-determination bases) from the Y2 observations and the declaration decl, then compute Trace, Part, HDyn, Q by D19.
    Three separate layers: observational facts (trajectory, knockout differences, local responses, read-outs); declarations and their certification status (decl); verdicts (values and the antecedents they consume).
    Rules (atom construction rules of Section 4.6):
      Act(m): 1 if |Δ_m| > ε, otherwise 0 (antecedent PROTO);
      HistCond(m): 1 if there is a local response at a certified available position (LOC exclusivity: evidence edge (i,t)→m set to 1);
                   0 if m is effective, a legitimate perturbation was applied at every candidate position and none responded (antecedent PROTO, plus the formation-negation antecedents of the excluded positions);
                   ⊥ if the response occurs only at positions of undetermined availability, or some candidate position was not perturbed or has no legitimate perturbation (insufficient perturbation coverage); ⊥ if m is ineffective and there is no response;
      Cond(m): as above; for an ineffective process with declared OUT-f it is decided by the read-out response, otherwise ⊥_Sub;
      Form, Mark: 1 once seen; if not seen, 0 only under PROTO, INV-c, REC, with ENT in addition for positions already above ε at the record start; otherwise ⊥;
      a 0 for Part or HDyn additionally requires INV-c; Ident and HistEv are given by POS and REC.
    pert (Lab): the perturbation applied at each position; 'none' (declared: no legitimate nonzero perturbation) gives ⊥_Pert, 'unapplied' and an illegitimate applied candidate give ⊥_Obs; none of them can support a negative determination."""
    err = validate_mechanism_decl(lab, hist, decl, T)
    if err is not None:                        # an invalid declaration does not enter evaluation: every verdict is ⊥ and the reason code is reported
        res = {'trace': None, 'part': None, 'hdyn': None, 'Q': None, 'unexplained': None, 'dom': err,
               'basis': {'trace': frozenset(), 'part': frozenset(), 'hdyn': frozenset(), 'Q': frozenset()}}
        if detail: res.update(rows=[], edges=[], form={}, mark={})
        return res
    M = lab.M; eps = M.eps; T = tuple(T if T is not None else M.T)
    traj = trajectory(M, hist)
    inv = lab.inventory()
    pre = {x for x in M.sites if traj[0][x] > eps}
    form = {x: [] for x in M.sites}; marks = {x: [] for x in M.sites}
    def form_neg_basis(i):
        """Whether the formation negation of position i is certified; returns its basis if so, otherwise None."""
        if not (decl.proto and decl.inv_c and decl.rec): return None
        if i in pre and not decl.ent: return None
        return frozenset({'PROTO', 'INV-c', 'REC'} | ({'ENT'} if i in pre else set()))
    def form_unk_code(i):
        """Reason codes for an uncertified formation negation: missing PROTO gives ⊥_Prof, missing INV-c gives ⊥_Inv, missing REC or (for a position present at the start) missing ENT gives ⊥_Rec."""
        c = set()
        if not decl.proto: c.add('⊥_Prof')
        if not decl.inv_c: c.add('⊥_Inv')
        if not decl.rec or (i in pre and not decl.ent): c.add('⊥_Rec')
        return frozenset(c)
    def pert_code(i, s):
        d = lab.delta(i, s)
        if d == 'none': return '⊥_Pert'                   # D_i(s) declared empty: no legitimate nonzero perturbation
        return '⊥_Obs'                                    # exists but not applied, or the applied candidate is illegitimate (negative set value or one-step successor): no legitimate response obtained; D_i(s) is not inferred to be empty
    parts = []; hd = []; unexplained = False; rows = []; edges = []
    for idx, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        s = traj[idx]; full = lab.full(t, s, u)
        explained = {x: Fr(0) for x in M.sites}
        if decl.proto:
            for x in M.sites:                                   # crossing the threshold is formation (F-class booking rule)
                if s[x] <= eps < traj[idx + 1][x]:
                    form[x].append(t)
        for j, (tg, _a) in enumerate(M.pulses_of(u)):       # knock out pulses one by one
            c = full[tg] - net(M, t, s, u, drop=f'pulse#{j}')[tg]
            explained[tg] += c
            if c > eps and decl.proto:
                form[tg].append(t); marks[tg].append(t)
        for name, inside, tg in inv:
            c = full[tg] - lab.ko(t, s, u, name)[tg]       # knockout difference = threshold-truncated booked term ĉ_m
            explained[tg] += c
            act = abs(c) > eps
            if decl.proto and act and c > eps and s[tg] <= eps:
                form[tg].append(t)
            if decl.proto and act and not inside:
                marks[tg].append(t)
            if t < M.t0:
                continue
            if not decl.proto:
                UP = U('⊥_Prof')                            # atomic adjudication protocols not declared: only state-level evidence
                rows.append((name, t, inside, tg, c, UP, UP, UP)); parts.append(UP) if inside else None; hd.append(UP)
                continue
            vact = V1 if act else V0('PROTO')
            cand = [i for i in T if s[i] > eps]
            known, unk, excl = [], [], {}
            for i in cand:
                if any(f < t for f in form[i]): known.append(i)
                else:
                    b = form_neg_basis(i)
                    if b is None: unk.append(i)
                    else: excl[i] = b
            def response(i, fn):
                d = lab.delta(i, s)
                if d in ('none', 'unapplied'): return None
                if s[i] + d < 0: return None
                s2 = lab.set_local(s, i, s[i] + d)
                if any(v < 0 for v in lab.full(t, s2, u).values()): return None   # the one-step successor after the setting leaves the F domain: not a legitimate perturbation
                return fn(s2)
            ko_val = lambda s2: lab.full(t, s2, u)[tg] - lab.ko(t, s2, u, name)[tg]
            resp = {i: (lambda r: None if r is None else r != c)(response(i, ko_val)) for i in cand}
            rk = [i for i in known if resp[i] is True]
            if rk:
                vhc = V1
                edges += [(f'{i}@{t}', f'{name}@{t}') for i in rk]
            elif not cand:
                vhc = V0('PROTO')
            elif act:
                if any(resp[i] is True for i in unk) or any(resp[i] is None for i in known + unk):
                    vhc = (None, frozenset().union(*[form_unk_code(i) for i in unk if resp[i] is True],
                                                   [pert_code(i, s) for i in known + unk if resp[i] is None]))
                else:
                    b = {'PROTO'}
                    for i, bx in excl.items():
                        if resp[i] is not False: b |= bx       # if an excluded position responds or was not perturbed, the 0 depends on its formation negation
                    vhc = (False, frozenset(b))
            else:
                vhc = U('⊥_Sub')                               # ineffective process with no response
            o = lab.out(t, s, u, name)
            running = _runs(lab, t, u, name)
            if not running:
                vcond = V0('PROTO')
            elif not cand:
                vcond = V0('PROTO')
            elif act:
                vcond = vhc
            elif rk:
                vcond = V1
            elif o is not None and name in decl.outf:
                orsp = {i: (lambda r: None if r is None else r != o)(response(i, lambda s2: lab.out(t, s2, u, name))) for i in cand}
                if any(orsp[i] is True for i in known):
                    vcond = V1
                elif any(orsp[i] is True for i in unk) or any(orsp[i] is None for i in known + unk):
                    vcond = (None, frozenset().union(*[form_unk_code(i) for i in unk if orsp[i] is True],
                                                     [pert_code(i, s) for i in known + unk if orsp[i] is None]))
                else:
                    b = {'PROTO', 'OUT-f'}
                    for i, bx in excl.items():
                        if orsp[i] is not False: b |= bx
                    vcond = (False, frozenset(b))
            else:
                vcond = U('⊥_Sub')
            if inside:
                parts.append(vcond)
            hd.append(AND(V1 if tg in T else V0('PROTO'), vact, vhc))
            rows.append((name, t, inside, tg, c, vact, vcond, vhc))
        for x in M.sites:
            if full[x] - s[x] != explained[x]:
                unexplained = True
    end = traj[-1]
    def found(lst, x):
        if lst: return V1
        b = form_neg_basis(x)
        return (None, form_unk_code(x)) if b is None else (False, b)
    elig = AND(*[AND(V1 if end[i] > eps else V0(), found(form[i], i)) for i in T])
    src = OR(*[found(marks[i], i) for i in T])
    vtrace = AND(src, elig, V1 if decl.pos else U('⊥_Id'), V1 if decl.rec else U('⊥_Rec'))
    def closed_or(xs):
        v = OR(*xs) if xs else (False, frozenset())
        if v[0] is False:
            return (False, v[1] | {'INV-c'}) if decl.inv_c else U('⊥_Inv')
        return v
    vpart = closed_or(parts); vhd = closed_or(hd)
    vq = AND(vtrace, vpart, vhd)
    config = {'T': T, 'ε': M.eps, 'declared period': (M.t0, M.t1), 'input history': tuple(hist),
              'perturbation setting': tuple(sorted((i, str(d)) for i, d in lab.pert.items())) or 'default +1 at every position',
              'test bench': ('MOD' if lab.modular else 'knockout triggers compensation') + ('' if lab.side is None else '; coupled setting') + ('' if not lab.hidden else '; hidden processes')}
    res = {'trace': vtrace[0], 'part': vpart[0], 'hdyn': vhd[0], 'Q': vq[0], 'unexplained': unexplained, 'dom': None, 'config': config,
           'basis': {'trace': vtrace[1], 'part': vpart[1], 'hdyn': vhd[1], 'Q': vq[1]}}
    if detail:
        res['rows'] = rows; res['edges'] = edges
        res['form'] = {i: found(form[i], i) for i in T}; res['mark'] = {i: found(marks[i], i) for i in T}
    return res

# ---------- 4.1 five-tuple report adapter ----------
from collections import namedtuple
Det = namedtuple('Det', 'v Code Level Dom Read')
_DECL_NAMES = (('proto', 'SEP, MOD, LOC, BEAT, INV-a'), ('inv_c', 'INV-c'), ('rec', 'REC'), ('ent', 'ENT'), ('pos', 'POS'), ('ef4', 'EF4'))
def report(res, decl, key, read=None):
    """Write one verdict of identify as the five-tuple Det(v, Code, Level, Dom, Read) of 4.1.
    v ∈ {1, 0, '⊥'}; Code: "witness" for 1, "0: antecedents …" for 0 (the antecedent list B), the reason codes for ⊥;
    Level is Y₂; Dom is the declared protocols and antecedents together with the effective configuration (T, ε, declared period, input history, perturbation setting, test bench); Read is the reading (by default the evidence edges and the unexplained-residual flag).
    For an invalid declaration v = '⊥' and Code is ⊥_Dom with the reason."""
    dom = {'declared protocols and antecedents': tuple(n for a, n in _DECL_NAMES if getattr(decl, a)) + ((('OUT-f: ' + ', '.join(sorted(decl.outf))),) if decl.outf else ()),
           'effective configuration': res.get('config')}
    if res.get('dom'):
        return Det('⊥', res['dom'][0] + ': ' + res['dom'][1], 'Y₂', dom, read)
    v, b = res[key], res['basis'][key]
    if v is True: val, code = 1, 'witness'
    elif v is False: val, code = 0, '0: antecedents ' + ', '.join(sorted(b))
    else: val, code = '⊥', ', '.join(sorted(b)) or '⊥'
    if read is None:
        read = {'unexplained residual': res['unexplained']}
        if 'edges' in res: read['evidence edges'] = tuple(res['edges'])
    return Det(val, code, 'Y₂', dom, read)
def report_value(res, decl, kind=None, key=None):
    """Five-tuple of a single atom, from the same source as the composite report: the atom is taken by type and key from the result res of the same identify call (detail=True required);
    Dom carries the actual configuration of that adjudication, and Read records the source context (type, position or process name, and beat index).
    kind ∈ {'Form', 'Mark'}: key is a position; kind ∈ {'Act', 'Cond', 'HistCond'}: key is (process name, beat); kind = 'Selfloop': in-boundary self-feedback type.
    If res does not contain the requested atom, the value is ⊥_Undef; the configuration is not filled in from another instance.
    Given only a single (v, B) without an adjudication result (the old calling form), there is no source context: the value is ⊥_Undef, and no empty configuration is passed off as a complete report."""
    if isinstance(res, tuple) or kind is None:
        return Det('⊥', '⊥_Undef: missing source context (the result of the same identify call, the atom type and the key must be given)', 'Y₂',
                   {'declared protocols and antecedents': None, 'effective configuration': 'missing source context'}, None)
    if res.get('dom'):
        return report(res, decl, 'Q', read={'type': kind, 'object': key})
    if kind in ('Form', 'Mark'):
        vb = res.get(kind.lower(), {}).get(key)
    elif kind in ('Act', 'Cond', 'HistCond'):
        col = {'Act': 5, 'Cond': 6, 'HistCond': 7}[kind]
        vb = next((r[col] for r in res.get('rows', []) if (r[0], r[1]) == key), None)
    elif kind == 'Selfloop':
        vb = selfloop_noext(res, decl) if 'rows' in res else None
    else:
        vb = None
    read = {'type': kind, 'object': key}
    if vb is None:
        return Det('⊥', '⊥_Undef: no such atom in the given adjudication result', 'Y₂', {'declared protocols and antecedents': None, 'effective configuration': res.get('config')}, read)
    return report({'x': vb[0], 'basis': {'x': vb[1]}, 'unexplained': res.get('unexplained'), 'config': res.get('config')}, decl, 'x', read)
def selfloop_noext(res, decl):
    """Restricted implementation of the in-boundary self-feedback verdict of Section 4.8: it applies only to models with no out-of-boundary component and no out-of-boundary pathway (as in this model class),
    where the external clamp LOC coincides with LOC. Instances with an out-of-boundary pathway do not use this function; they get ⊥ or a separately implemented external-clamp intervention.
    1: some process in B has Act = HistCond = 1; 0: under INV-c and EF4, every running process in B has Act or HistCond already determined as 0; ⊥: otherwise.
    Returns (v, basis)."""
    rows = [r for r in res['rows'] if r[2]]
    v = OR(*[AND(r[5], r[7]) for r in rows]) if rows else (False, frozenset())
    if v[0] is False:
        if decl.inv_c and decl.ef4:
            return (False, v[1] | {'INV-c', 'EF4'})
        return U(*(['⊥_Inv'] if not decl.inv_c else []) + (['⊥_Undef'] if not decl.ef4 else []))
    return v

def formation(M, hist):
    """Formation record (same rule as Mathematical Form D16 and field_final): an effective pulse, or an effective mechanism component that takes the position above ε."""
    eps = M.eps; traj = trajectory(M, hist); form = {x: [] for x in M.sites}
    for idx, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        s = traj[idx]
        for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u):
            if exact(a) > eps:
                form[tg].append(t)
        for m in M.mechs:
            if m.runs(t, u):
                c = m.value(s, u)
                if abs(c) > eps and c > eps and s[m.target] <= eps:
                    form[m.target].append(t)
    return form

def t_max(M, hist):
    end = trajectory(M, hist)[-1]; form = formation(M, hist)
    return tuple(x for x in M.sites if end[x] > M.eps and form[x])

def _runs(lab, t, u, name):
    m = next(mm for mm in lab.M.mechs if mm.name == name)
    return m.runs(t, u)
~~~

A.5  experiments.py  Verifications C1–C17 of the second-layer criteria (Theorems 5–7, Lemma 6.1, Lemmas 8–12, 4.2 declaration entry, 4.6, 4.8, 4.9 report)

~~~python
"""The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field, Appendix A.5: verification of the second-layer criteria, C1–C17. Run: python3 experiments.py (with mtm.py and field_final.py in the same directory)"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from mtm import Mech, MTM, Lab, truth, trajectory, same_profile, hist_W, dep0, identify, net, full_decl, strip, selfloop_noext, Decl, report, report_value
def sl(res, decl):
    v = selfloop_noext(res, decl)[0]
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: None}[v]
import itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import sys
import os as _os
sys.path.insert(0, _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # field_final.py sits in the same directory as this file (identical, character for character, to The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A.1)
from field_final import run
H = Fr(1, 2)
ok_all = True
def check(cond, msg):
    global ok_all
    print(('  pass  ' if cond else '  fail  ') + msg)
    ok_all = ok_all and cond
def all_histories(M, alphabet):
    return itertools.product(alphabet, repeat=len(M.beats()))
def level1_equal(M1, M2, alphabet):
    """Full Y1 family: identical trajectories for every input history, and identical basic dependence effects on the sampled states.
       The two models are compared under a common frozen C_op: the same ε, alphabet, history set (every input history in W), sampled states and set values (0, 1)."""
    for h in all_histories(M1, alphabet):
        if trajectory(M1, list(h)) != trajectory(M2, list(h)):
            return False
    for t in M1.beats():
        for u in alphabet:
            for vals in itertools.product([Fr(0), H, Fr(1), Fr(2)], repeat=len(M1.sites)):
                s = dict(zip(M1.sites, vals))
                if net(M1, t, s, u) != net(M2, t, s, u):
                    return False
                for x in M1.sites:
                    for y in M1.sites:
                        if dep0(M1, t, s, u, x, y, 0, 1) != dep0(M2, t, s, u, x, y, 0, 1):
                            return False
    return True
# ---------------- C1 Profile equivalence with differing duties (Theorem 5(1)) ----------------
print('C1  Profile equivalence with differing duties')
scan_in = Mech('scan', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(0), Fr(0), (), (1,))
scan_out = Mech('scan', 'x', False, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(0), Fr(0), (), (1,))
base = dict(sites=('x',), s0=(0,), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=1, pulses=(('w', (('x', 1),)),))
alph = ('rest', 'w')
# (a) action: +a and −a cancel — Act/HDyn differ
A1 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in,))
B1 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in, Mech('m1', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), (), (1,)),
                        Mech('m2', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, Fr(0), (), (1,))))
h = ['w', 'rest']
check(same_profile(A1, B1, alph) and level1_equal(A1, B1, alph), '(a) cancelling pair: profiles identical; full-family Y1 readings equal under the common C_op')
check(truth(A1, h)['hdyn'] is False and truth(B1, h)['hdyn'] is True, '(a) cancelling pair: HDyn is 0 for one, 1 for the other')
# (b) issuing from the Trace-Response: a constant-law write vs a Trace-Response-dependent write plus a Trace-Response-dependent offset — HistCond/HDyn differ
A2 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in, Mech('c', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), H, (), (1,))))
B2 = MTM(**base, mechs=(scan_in, Mech('m1', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), (), (1,)),
                        Mech('m2', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, H, (), (1,))))
check(same_profile(A2, B2, alph) and level1_equal(A2, B2, alph), '(b) source pair: profiles identical; full-family Y1 readings equal under the common C_op')
check(truth(A2, h)['hdyn'] is False and truth(B2, h)['hdyn'] is True, '(b) source pair: HDyn is 0 for one, 1 for the other')
# (c) the signal leaves a trace: out-of-boundary amplification (+x/2, the signal leaves an effective change on x) vs a gated stop of shallowing (the signal only stops the shallowing mechanism) — r differs
seed = Mech('seed', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(1), (), (0,))
decay_amp = Mech('decay', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, Fr(0))
amp = Mech('amp', 'x', False, 'linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), (('rest', 0),))
decay_gate = Mech('decay', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), -H, Fr(0), (('a', 0),))
reset_g = Mech('reset', 'g', True, 'linear', ('g',), Fr(-1), Fr(0))
cbase = dict(sites=('x', 'g'), s0=(0, 0), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=2, pulses=(('a', (('g', 1),)),))
A3 = MTM(**cbase, mechs=(seed, decay_amp, amp, reset_g))
B3 = MTM(**cbase, mechs=(seed, decay_gate, reset_g))
h3 = ['rest', 'a', 'rest']
alph3 = ('rest', 'a')
check(same_profile(A3, B3, alph3) and level1_equal(A3, B3, alph3), '(c) trace-leaving pair: profiles identical; full-family Y1 readings equal under the common C_op')
check(hist_W(A3, h3, {1}) and hist_W(B3, h3, {1}), '(c) trace-leaving pair: window-end reading Hist is 1 for both')
check(truth(A3, h3)['trace'] is True and truth(B3, h3)['trace'] is False, '(c) trace-leaving pair: TraceSet (r) is 1 for one, 0 for the other')
# (d) participation: carrier of the read-out process inside B vs outside the boundary — Part differs

# (e) Fld via the maximal Trace-Response set: for each of the four pairs the ∃T conclusions also differ
def fld(M, hist):
    sites = M.sites; best = False
    for r in range(1, len(sites) + 1):
        for T in itertools.combinations(sites, r):
            q = truth(M, hist, T)['Q']
            if q is True: return True
            if q is None: best = None
    return best
check(all(fld(A, hh) != fld(B, hh) for A, B, hh in [(A1, B1, h), (A2, B2, h), (A3, B3, h3), (A4, B4, h)]),
      '(e) ∃T Q differs for all four pairs: Fld cannot be determined from Y1 either')
# ---------------- C2 Mechanism-level identification (Theorem 6): randomized regression ----------------
print('C2  Mechanism-level identification: F atoms reconstructed under SEP, MOD, LOC, BEAT, INV-a')
rng = random.Random(20261029)
def rand_mtm(rng):
    n = rng.randint(1, 3); sites = tuple('xyz'[:n])
    T = tuple(sorted(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, n))))
    eps = rng.choice([Fr(0), Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 2)])
    alphabet = ('rest', 'a', 'b')
    pulses = tuple((u, tuple((rng.choice(sites), rng.choice([Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1), Fr(2)]))
                             for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 2)))) for u in ('a', 'b'))
    mechs = []
    for i in range(rng.randint(1, 5)):
        law = rng.choice(['constant', 'linear', 'bilinear'])
        if law == 'constant': reads = ()
        elif law == 'linear': reads = tuple(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, n)))
        else: reads = (rng.choice(sites), rng.choice(sites))
        k = Fr(rng.choice([-1, 0, 1, 2]), rng.choice([1, 2, 4]))
        b = Fr(rng.choice([-1, 0, 0, 1, 2]), rng.choice([1, 2, 4]))
        if law == 'constant' and rng.random() < 0.3: b = Fr(0)
        gate = tuple((u, rng.choice([0, 1, 1, 2])) for u in alphabet if rng.random() < 0.4)
        beats = None if rng.random() < 0.6 else tuple(sorted(rng.sample(range(0, 3), rng.randint(1, 3))))
        mechs.append(Mech(f'm{i}', rng.choice(sites), rng.random() < 0.7, law, reads, k, b, gate, beats))
    M = MTM(sites, tuple(rng.choice([0, 0, 1]) for _ in sites), T, eps, 1, 2, pulses, tuple(mechs))
    hist = [rng.choice(alphabet) for _ in M.beats()]
    return M, hist
n_ok = n_tot = 0; n_pos = 0; n_und = 0; n_pert = 0
while n_tot < 600:
    M, hist = rand_mtm(rng)
    try:
        tr = truth(M, hist)
    except ValueError:
        continue          # outside the F domain (negative depth, etc.)
    idr = identify(Lab(M), hist, full_decl(M))
    n_tot += 1
    def leq(a, b):   # information order: a ⪯ b
        return a is None or a == b
    sound = all(leq(idr[k], tr[k]) for k in ('trace', 'part', 'hdyn', 'Q')) and not idr['unexplained']
    pert_only = idr['hdyn'] is None and idr['basis']['hdyn'] == frozenset({'⊥_Obs'})    # the successor under the applied default setting leaves F: no legitimate response obtained
    n_pert += pert_only
    exact_hd = (idr['hdyn'] == tr['hdyn'] or pert_only) and idr['trace'] == tr['trace']
    n_ok += sound and exact_hd; n_pos += (tr['Q'] is True)
    n_und += (idr['Q'] is None)
    if not (sound and exact_hd):
        print('   counterexample', M, hist, tr, idr); break
check(n_ok == n_tot, f'random {n_tot} cases: the reconstructed Trace equals the upstream evaluator item by item; HDyn is equal, or is ⊥_Obs because the applied default setting is illegitimate ({n_pert} cases: the default +1 setting takes the one-step successor outside the F domain, so no legitimate response was obtained); Part and Q never exceed the truth value in the information order (Q is ⊥ in {n_und} cases: at that beat a constant-law or bilinear-law process has no effective component and no read output, so its read dependence is invisible at the given resolution, or no legitimate response was obtained); Q = 1 in {n_pos} cases; the closure check finds no residual anywhere')
check(all(identify(Lab(M), hh, full_decl(M))['Q'] == truth(M, hh)['Q'] for M, hh in [(A1, h), (B1, h), (A2, h), (B2, h), (A3, h3), (B3, h3), (A4, h), (B4, h)]),
~~~

~~~python
# ---------------- C4 Reduction to the maximal Trace-Response set (Lemma 9) ----------------
print('C4  Reduction to the maximal Trace-Response set')
from mtm import to_spec, t_max, formation
def tri_max(vals):
    return True if True in vals else (None if None in vals else False)
cnt = 0; agree = 0; nonempty = 0
rng2 = random.Random(7)
while cnt < 600:
    M, hist = rand_mtm(rng2)
    ident = rng2.choice([True, True, None])
    try:
        truth(M, hist)
    except ValueError:
        continue
    vals = []
    for k in range(1, len(M.sites) + 1):
        for T in itertools.combinations(M.sites, k):
            sp = to_spec(M, hist, T); sp = sp.__class__(**{**sp.__dict__, 'identity': ident})
            vals.append(run(sp)['Q'])
    Tm = t_max(M, hist)
    if Tm:
        sp = to_spec(M, hist, Tm); sp = sp.__class__(**{**sp.__dict__, 'identity': ident})
        qm = run(sp)['Q']; nonempty += 1
    else:
        qm = False
    cnt += 1
    agree += (tri_max(vals) == qm)
check(agree == cnt, f'random {cnt} cases (T_max nonempty in {nonempty} cases; the cross-time identity atom takes 1 or ⊥): in the truth order max_T Q(T) = Q(T_max); when T_max is empty, Q is 0 for every T')

from mtm import trajectory
cnt = bad = 0
rng3 = random.Random(11)

    M, hist = rand_mtm(rng3)

        truth(M, hist)
    except ValueError:
        continue
    traj = trajectory(M, hist); form = formation(M, hist)
    for idx, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        if t < M.t0: continue
        s = traj[idx]
        for x in M.T:
            acting = [m for m in M.mechs if m.target == x and m.runs(t, u)]
            pulsed = any(tg == x for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u) if a > M.eps)
            if len(acting) != 1 or pulsed: continue      # SAM: exactly one event acts on x at this beat
            m = acting[0]
            for y in M.T:
                if not (s[y] > M.eps and any(f < t for f in form[y])): continue
                lemma = dep0(M, t, s, u, x, y, s[y], s[y] + 1) and abs(net(M, t, s, u)[x] - s[x]) > M.eps
                c = m.value(s, u)
                truth_w = abs(c) > M.eps and (m.value({**s, y: s[y] + 1}, u) != c)
                cnt += 1; bad += (lemma != truth_w)
check(bad == 0, f'Dep₀ bridge under SAM: over {cnt} (beat, x, y) instances, “Dep₀ ∧ effective net change” equals “Act of the unique acting mechanism ∧ functional support contains y” case by case')
~~~

~~~python
# Hist and r: bridge and gating counterexample under pure pulse entry (PE)
print('C5′  Hist (window-end reading) and r')
cnt = bad = 0
rng4 = random.Random(5)
while cnt < 800:
    M, hist = rand_mtm(rng4)
    # PE: no mechanism with an out-of-boundary carrier, no input gating — the signal enters only as pulses
    M = MTM(M.sites, M.s0, M.T, M.eps, M.t0, M.t1, M.pulses,
            tuple(Mech(m.name, m.target, True, m.law, m.reads, m.k, m.b, (), m.beats) for m in M.mechs))
    try:
        tr = truth(M, hist)
    except ValueError:
        continue
    end = trajectory(M, hist)[-1]

    seg_hit = False
    for i, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        ent = [tg for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u)]          # entry positions are read from the declaration, not from the pulse amounts
        if ent and all(tg in M.T for tg in ent) and hist_W(M, hist, {i}):
            seg_hit = True
    if seg_hit:
        cnt += 1
        form = formation(M, hist)
        # The bridge claims only r (an effective change left by the signal on T); the ground truth is read out through Mark
        marks_T = any(tg in M.T and a > M.eps for t, u in zip(M.beats(), hist) for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u))
        bad += (not marks_T)

# ---------------- C6 First-layer negative determination is impossible: three twin constructions (Theorem 5(2)(3)) ----------------
print('C6  First-layer twin constructions (profile unchanged, duty made to hold)')
from mtm import formation
def grid_equal(M1, M2, alphabet):
    for t in M1.beats():
        for u in alphabet:
            for vals in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(1, 3), Fr(1), Fr(5, 2)], repeat=len(M1.sites)):
                st = dict(zip(M1.sites, vals))
                if net(M1, t, st, u) != net(M2, t, st, u):
                    return False
    return all(trajectory(M1, list(hh)) == trajectory(M2, list(hh)) for hh in itertools.product(alphabet, repeat=len(M1.beats())))
rng5 = random.Random(99)
n = good = n_av = 0
while n < 300:
    M, hist = rand_mtm(rng5)
    try:
        truth(M, hist)
    except ValueError:
        continue
    traj = trajectory(M, hist); form = formation(M, hist)
    x = M.T[0]; a = M.eps + 1
    # r twin: out-of-boundary constant +a and in-boundary constant −a, acting on x at the same beat
    Mr = MTM(M.sites, M.s0, M.T, M.eps, M.t0, M.t1, M.pulses,
             M.mechs + (Mech('tw_e', x, False, 'constant', (), Fr(0), a, (), (M.t0,)),
                        Mech('tw_i', x, True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), -a, (), (M.t0,))))
    # Precondition visible at the first layer: s_t(y) > ε for some beat in W and some y ∈ T (the formation record itself is not a first-layer visible quantity)
    cand = [(idx, t, y) for idx, t in enumerate(M.beats()) if t >= M.t0 for y in M.T if traj[idx][y] > M.eps]
    ok = grid_equal(M, Mr, ('rest', 'a', 'b'))
    tr_r = run(to_spec(Mr, hist))['trace']
    end = traj[-1]
    elig = all(end[z] > M.eps and form[z] for z in M.T)
    ok = ok and (tr_r is True if elig else True)
    if cand:
        n_av += 1
        idx, t, y = cand[0]
        base_mechs = M.mechs; base_pulses = M.pulses
        if not any(f < t for f in form[y]):
            # Supply a formation record: under the entry-beat input u0 add a pulse +a acting on y, offset by an in-boundary constant law −a that runs only under u0 (profile unchanged)
            u0 = hist[0]
            alph = ('rest', 'a', 'b')
            base_pulses = tuple((uu, ps + ((y, a),)) if uu == u0 else (uu, ps) for uu, ps in M.pulses)
            if u0 not in [uu for uu, _ in M.pulses]:
                base_pulses = base_pulses + ((u0, ((y, a),)),)
            base_mechs = base_mechs + (Mech('tw_c', y, True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), -a,
                                            tuple((v, 0) for v in alph if v != u0)),)
        sy = traj[idx][y]
        k = (M.eps + 1) / sy + 1
        Mh = MTM(M.sites, M.s0, M.T, M.eps, M.t0, M.t1, base_pulses,
                 base_mechs + (Mech('tw1', x, True, 'linear', (y,), k, Fr(0), (), (t,)),
                               Mech('tw2', x, True, 'linear', (y,), -k, Fr(0), (), (t,))))
        Mp = MTM(M.sites, M.s0, M.T, M.eps, M.t0, M.t1, base_pulses,
                 base_mechs + (Mech('tw_r', y, True, 'linear', (y,), Fr(0), Fr(0), (), (t,)),))
        ok = ok and grid_equal(M, Mh, ('rest', 'a', 'b')) and grid_equal(M, Mp, ('rest', 'a', 'b'))
        try:
            ok = ok and run(to_spec(Mh, hist))['hdyn'] is True and run(to_spec(Mp, hist))['part'] is True
        except ValueError:
            ok = False
    else:
        # Every position of T stays ≤ ε within W: in every twin the available positions are empty, Part = HDyn = 0, and the first layer may issue 0
        tr = truth(M, hist)
        ok = ok and tr['part'] is False and tr['hdyn'] is False
    n += 1; good += ok
check(good == n, f'random {n} cases: the r twin leaves the profile unchanged and Src = 1; in the {n_av} cases where some position of T exceeds ε within W, the HDyn twin and the Part twin leave the profile unchanged and the duty is 1; in the remaining cases Part = HDyn = 0')
# First-layer criterion for q: retained at window end and ≤ ε at some point in the record ⇒ Form ≠ ∅ in every F decomposition

while n < 600:

check(good == n, f'{n} positions that are > ε at window end and were ≤ ε at some point in the record: every formation record is nonempty (a threshold crossing always involves an individually effective component)')
# ---------------- C7 Local response and functional support (Theorem 6(2)) ----------------
print('C7  Local response of the threshold-truncated knockout difference and D15 functional support')
def supp_d15(law, reads, k, st):
    if law == 'constant' or k == 0: return set()
    if law == 'linear': return set(reads)
    j1, j2 = reads
    return ({j1} if st[j2] != 0 else set()) | ({j2} if st[j1] != 0 else set())
def val(law, reads, k, b, st):
    if law == 'constant': return b
    if law == 'linear': return b + k * sum(st[r] for r in reads)
    return b + k * st[reads[0]] * st[reads[1]]
def chat(c, eps):            # observable: the threshold-truncated booked term (knockout difference)
    return c if abs(c) > eps else Fr(0)
rng7 = random.Random(1029)
n_eff = n_ineff = n_cross = n_bad = n_sq = 0
N_PROC = 20000
for _ in range(N_PROC):
    sites = ('x', 'y', 'z'); eps = rng7.choice([Fr(0), Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)])
    law = rng7.choice(['constant', 'linear', 'bilinear', 'bilinear'])
    reads = () if law == 'constant' else (tuple(rng7.sample(sites, rng7.randint(1, 3))) if law == 'linear'
                                           else (rng7.choice(sites), rng7.choice(sites)))
    k = Fr(rng7.choice([-3, -1, 0, 1, 2, 5]), rng7.choice([1, 2, 7, 100]))
    b = Fr(rng7.choice([-2, -1, 0, 1, 3]), rng7.choice([1, 2, 10]))
    st = {x: Fr(rng7.choice([0, 1, 2, 3, 5, 9]), rng7.choice([1, 2, 10])) for x in sites}
    c0 = val(law, reads, k, b, st); eff = abs(c0) > eps
    for i in sites:
        if not st[i] > eps: continue                       # judge only at available positions (s(i) > ε)
        for d in {-st[i], -st[i] / 2, Fr(-1, 1000), Fr(1, 1000), Fr(1), Fr(7)}:
            if d == 0 or st[i] + d < 0: continue           # legitimate perturbation: δ ≠ 0 and the state after the setting is not negative
            s2 = dict(st); s2[i] = st[i] + d
            c1 = val(law, reads, k, b, s2)
            resp = chat(c1, eps) != chat(c0, eps)
            insupp = i in supp_d15(law, reads, k, st)
            if law == 'bilinear' and reads[0] == reads[1] == i: n_sq += 1
            if eff:
                n_eff += 1; n_bad += (resp != insupp)
            else:
                n_ineff += 1
                if resp:
                    n_cross += 1; n_bad += (not insupp)
check(n_bad == 0, f'{N_PROC} random processes: over {n_eff} perturbations of effective processes, response ⇔ i ∈ Supp_act (including {n_sq} same-position bilinear ones and the δ = −s boundary); of {n_ineff} perturbations of ineffective processes, {n_cross} gave threshold-crossing responses, all with i ∈ Supp_act')
# Critical case: c(s) = ε exactly at the threshold (ineffective); the threshold is crossed after the perturbation
eps = Fr(1, 2); st = {'x': Fr(1)}
c0 = val('linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), st); c1 = val('linear', ('x',), H, Fr(0), {'x': Fr(2)})
check(c0 == eps and chat(c0, eps) == 0 and chat(c1, eps) == 1 and 'x' in supp_d15('linear', ('x',), H, st),
~~~

~~~python
# ---------------- C15 Negative-determination bases (Lemma 6.1, 4.6) ----------------
print('C15  Antecedent list of every 0 verdict: with the observational facts fixed, withdrawing any one item ends the 0 verdict')
# Fixity premise of Lemma 6.1: the same Lab (same model, same perturbation setting), the same input history, the same T — observational facts, candidate positions and event sets do not change;
# withdrawing a declaration changes only decl; it neither reselects observations nor shrinks a quantifier domain. Covers Form, Mark, Act, Cond, HistCond, Trace, Part, HDyn, Q item by item.
rng8 = random.Random(2468)
n_inst = 0; n_y1 = 0; bad_y1 = 0
ALL = {'PROTO', 'INV-c', 'REC', 'ENT', 'POS', 'EF4', 'OUT-f'}
KINDS = ('Form', 'Mark', 'Act', 'Cond', 'HistCond', 'Trace', 'Part', 'HDyn', 'Q')
cnt = {k: [0, 0, 0] for k in KINDS}        # number of 0 verdicts, number of withdrawals, number of times still 0 after withdrawal or with an antecedent outside the declaration items
def atoms(r):
    """Expand the result of identify into {(type, key): (v, B)}; the key set is the same for the same Lab and history."""
    out = {}
    for i, v in r['form'].items(): out[('Form', i)] = v
    for i, v in r['mark'].items(): out[('Mark', i)] = v
    for (name, t, inside, tg, c, va, vc, vh) in r['rows']:
        out[('Act', (name, t))] = va; out[('Cond', (name, t))] = vc; out[('HistCond', (name, t))] = vh
    for key, kind in (('trace', 'Trace'), ('part', 'Part'), ('hdyn', 'HDyn'), ('Q', 'Q')):
        out[(kind, None)] = (r[key], r['basis'][key])
    return out
while n_inst < 300:
    M, hist = rand_mtm(rng8)
    try:
        truth(M, hist)
    except ValueError:
        continue
    n_inst += 1
    lab = Lab(M); D = full_decl(M); A = atoms(identify(lab, hist, D, detail=True))
    stripped = {}
    for (kind, key), (v, b) in A.items():
        if v is not False: continue
        cnt[kind][0] += 1
        if not b <= ALL: cnt[kind][2] += 1
        for X in b:
            cnt[kind][1] += 1
            if X not in stripped: stripped[X] = atoms(identify(lab, hist, strip(D, X), detail=True))
            if stripped[X][(kind, key)][0] is False: cnt[kind][2] += 1
    r0 = identify(lab, hist, Decl())
    for key in ('trace', 'part', 'hdyn', 'Q'):
        if r0[key] is False:
            n_y1 += 1; bad_y1 += bool(r0['basis'][key])
for kind in KINDS:
    z, nr, bd = cnt[kind]
    check(bd == 0 and z > 0, f'{kind}: {z} verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, {nr} withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items')
tz = sum(c[0] for c in cnt.values()); tr = sum(c[1] for c in cnt.values())
f4 = [cnt[k] for k in ('Trace', 'Part', 'HDyn', 'Q')]
check(all(c[2] == 0 for c in cnt.values()), f'random {n_inst} cases in total: {tz} verdicts of 0, {tr} withdrawals (of these, Trace, Part, HDyn and Q together account for {sum(c[0] for c in f4)} verdicts and {sum(c[1] for c in f4)} withdrawals); observational facts, candidate positions and event sets fixed')
check(bad_y1 == 0, f'no declaration supplied (Decl default, nothing declared): only {n_y1} verdicts of 0 remain, all of them Y₁ verdicts that consume no antecedent (not retained at window end)')
# ---------------- C16 Single entry point for mechanism-level declarations (4.2, Mathematical Form D20) ----------------
print('C16  Invalid mechanism-level declarations do not enter evaluation: ⊥, with the reason reported')
base = MTM(sites=('x', 'y'), s0=(0, 0), T=('x',), eps=Fr(1, 100), t0=1, t1=2,
           pulses=(('w', (('x', 1),)),), mechs=(Mech('m', 'y', True, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0)),))
hh = ['w', 'rest', 'rest']
check(identify(Lab(base), hh, full_decl(base))['dom'] is None, 'valid declaration: passes the entry point and is evaluated as usual')
import dataclasses as _dc
def _rej(Mx, hx):
    """Whether the upstream evaluator rejects the same input (ValueError)."""
    try: truth(Mx, hx)
    except ValueError: return True
    return False
cases = [
    ('ε negative', _dc.replace(base, eps=Fr(-1, 100)), hh, {}),
    ('duplicate position name', _dc.replace(base, sites=('x', 'x')), hh, {}),
    ('empty position names', _dc.replace(base, sites=(), s0=()), hh, {}),
    ('process lacks an action target', _dc.replace(base, mechs=(Mech('m', None, True, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0)),)), hh, {}),
    ('process run beat outside the declared window', _dc.replace(base, mechs=(Mech('m', 'y', True, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0), (), (5,)),)), hh, {}),
    ('read position not in the position set', _dc.replace(base, mechs=(Mech('m', 'y', True, 'linear', ('z',), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0)),)), hh, {}),
    ('t₀ > t₁', _dc.replace(base, t0=3, t1=2), hh, {}),
    ('input history length mismatch', base, ['w', 'rest'], {}),
    ('perturbation is 0', base, hh, {'x': Fr(0)}),
    ('invalid perturbation value', base, hh, {'x': 'big'}),
    ('perturbation position not in the position set', base, hh, {'z': Fr(1)}),
]
cases += [
    ('negative trajectory depth (constant shallowing −1 from 0)', _dc.replace(base, pulses=(), mechs=(Mech('drain', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(-1)),)), hh, {}),
    ('linear law with empty read positions', _dc.replace(base, mechs=(Mech('m', 'y', True, 'linear', (), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0)),)), hh, {}),
    ('constant law with read positions', _dc.replace(base, mechs=(Mech('m', 'y', True, 'constant', ('x',), Fr(0), Fr(1)),)), hh, {}),
    ('declared T duplicated', _dc.replace(base, T=('x', 'x')), hh, {}),
]
res_c = [(nm, identify(Lab(Mx, pert=pt), hx, full_decl(Mx) if Mx.mechs and all(m.target for m in Mx.mechs) else Decl())) for nm, Mx, hx, pt in cases]
check(all(r['dom'] is not None and r['dom'][0] == '⊥_Dom' and r['Q'] is None for nm, r in res_c),
      'family of erroneous declaration parameters (' + ', '.join(nm for nm, _ in res_c) + '): all give ⊥_Dom with a reason, and no unexplained exception is thrown; consistent with the rejections of the upstream evaluator')
neg = res_c[-4][1]['dom'][1]
check('beat' in neg and 'negative value outside the F domain' in neg and all(_rej(Mx, hx) for nm, Mx, hx, pt in cases[-4:]), f'negative depth reports the beat, position and value ({neg}); the upstream evaluator rejects the last four kinds as well')
Pd = MTM(sites=('x',), s0=(0,), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=1, pulses=(('w', (('x', 1),)),), mechs=(Mech('m', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(-1), (), (1,)),))
hcode = lambda lab: report_value(identify(lab, h, full_decl(Pd), detail=True), full_decl(Pd), 'HistCond', ('m', 1))
check(identify(Lab(Pd), h, full_decl(Pd))['dom'] is None and hcode(Lab(Pd)).v == 0 and hcode(Lab(Pd, pert={'x': Fr(-1, 2)})).Code == '⊥_Obs',
      'legitimate shallowing is evaluated as usual: no response to the +1 perturbation gives 0; the applied −1/2 makes the one-step successor negative, so it is not a legitimate perturbation and no legitimate response was obtained: ⊥_Obs (D_x is not inferred to be empty)')
Pq = MTM(sites=('x',), s0=(0,), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=1, pulses=(('w', (('x', 1),)),), mechs=(Mech('d', 'x', True, 'linear', ('x',), Fr(-2), Fr(1), (), (1,)),))
qcode = lambda lab: report_value(identify(lab, h, full_decl(Pq), detail=True), full_decl(Pq), 'HistCond', ('d', 1))
check((qcode(Lab(Pq)).Code, qcode(Lab(Pq, pert={'x': Fr(-1, 2)})).v, qcode(Lab(Pq, pert={'x': 'none'})).Code, qcode(Lab(Pq, pert={'x': 'unapplied'})).Code) == ('⊥_Obs', 1, '⊥_Pert', '⊥_Obs'),
      'perturbation causes kept apart: the applied default +1 is illegitimate (successor −1): ⊥_Obs, and D_x is not reported empty; the applied −1/2 is legitimate, HistCond = 1; D_x declared empty: ⊥_Pert; exists but not applied: ⊥_Obs')
# ---------------- C17 Five-tuple reports and ⊥ reason codes (4.1, 4.6, 4.9) ----------------
print('C17  Five-tuple reports of second-layer verdicts and ⊥ reason codes')
from mtm import report, report_value
def atom(lab, d, kind, nm='m', hh=None):
    """The result of a single identify call and, from the same source, the atom report of process nm in it."""
    res = identify(lab, hh or h, d, detail=True)
    key = next((rr[0], rr[1]) for rr in res['rows'] if rr[0] == nm)
    return report_value(res, d, kind, key)
rr = identify(Lab(Pm), h, Dp, detail=True); dq = report(rr, Dp, 'hdyn')
check(dq.v == 1 and dq.Code == 'witness' and dq.Level == 'Y₂' and 'INV-c' in dq.Dom['declared protocols and antecedents'] and 'evidence edges' in dq.Read,
      f'five-tuple of a verdict 1: v = 1, Code = witness, Level = Y₂, Dom lists the declared protocols, Read contains the evidence edges ({dq.Read["evidence edges"]})')
d0 = report(identify(Lab(Pc), h, full_decl(Pc), detail=True), full_decl(Pc), 'hdyn')
check(d0.v == 0 and d0.Code.startswith('0: antecedents') and 'INV-c' in d0.Code, f'five-tuple of a verdict 0: Code lists the antecedents ({d0.Code})')
codes = {
    '⊥_Pert (no legitimate nonzero perturbation)': atom(Lab(Pm, pert={'y': 'none', 'x': 'none'}), Dp, 'HistCond').Code,
    '⊥_Obs (perturbation exists but not applied)': atom(Lab(Pm, pert={'y': 'unapplied'}), Dp, 'HistCond').Code,
    '⊥_Inv (INV-c not declared)': report(identify(Lab(Pc), h, full_decl(Pc, inv_c=False)), full_decl(Pc, inv_c=False), 'hdyn').Code,
    '⊥_Rec (REC not declared)': report(identify(Lab(Pm), h, full_decl(Pm, rec=False)), full_decl(Pm, rec=False), 'trace').Code,
    '⊥_Id (POS not declared)': report(identify(Lab(Pm), h, full_decl(Pm, pos=False)), full_decl(Pm, pos=False), 'trace').Code,
    '⊥_Prof (atomic adjudication protocols not declared)': report(identify(Lab(Pm), h, Decl()), Decl(), 'hdyn').Code,
}
~~~

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;'reason codes for ⊥ generated class by class: ' + '; '.join(f'{k} → {v}' for k, v in codes.items()))  
Ps = MTM(sites=('x', 'y'), s0=(0, 0), T=('x', 'y'), eps=Fr(1, 2), t0=1, t1=1, pulses=(('w', (('x', 1), ('y', 1))),),  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mechs=(scan\_in, Mech('m', 'x', True, 'constant', (), Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), (), (1,))))  
sub = atom(Lab(Ps), full\_decl(Ps), 'HistCond').Code  
c1 = report(identify(Lab(Pm, pert={'y': Fr(-1, 2)}), h, Dp), Dp, 'hdyn').Dom\['effective configuration'\]  
c2 = report(identify(Lab(Pm), h, Dp), Dp, 'hdyn').Dom\['effective configuration'\]  
check(c1 != c2 and c1\['T'\] == ('x', 'y') and c1\['perturbation setting'\] == (('y', '-1/2'),) and c2\['perturbation setting'\] == 'default +1 at every position' and c1\['input history'\] == tuple(h),

~~~text
      f'Dom records the effective configuration: T, ε, declared period, input history, perturbation setting and test bench; a different perturbation setting gives a different Dom ({c1["perturbation setting"]} vs {c2["perturbation setting"]})')
check(sub == '⊥_Sub', f'ineffective process (|component| ≤ ε) with no response: the Code of HistCond = {sub}')
# Atom reports and composite reports share a source: the configuration of the same identify call; when the perturbation or the input history changes, the configuration of the atom report changes with it; a missing atom gives ⊥_Undef
r1 = identify(Lab(Pm), h, Dp, detail=True); r2 = identify(Lab(Pm, pert={'y': Fr(-1, 2)}), h, Dp, detail=True)
k1 = next((rr[0], rr[1]) for rr in r1['rows'] if rr[0] == 'm')
a1, a2 = report_value(r1, Dp, 'Act', k1), report_value(r2, Dp, 'Act', k1)
fa, ma, sa = report_value(r1, Dp, 'Form', 'x'), report_value(r1, Dp, 'Mark', 'y'), report_value(r1, Dp, 'Selfloop')
miss = report_value(r1, Dp, 'Act', ('nonexistent', 9))
legacy = report_value(r1['rows'][0][5], Dp)
check(a1.Dom['effective configuration'] == report(r1, Dp, 'part').Dom['effective configuration'] is not None and a1.Dom != a2.Dom
      and a1.Read == {'type': 'Act', 'object': k1} and all(x.Dom['effective configuration'] is not None for x in (fa, ma, sa)) and miss.Code.startswith('⊥_Undef') and legacy.v == '⊥' and legacy.Code.startswith('⊥_Undef: missing source context'),
      f'atom reports and composite reports share a source: the Dom of Act, Form, Mark and the in-boundary self-feedback type all carry the configuration of the same adjudication, and Read records the source ({a1.Read}); a different perturbation setting gives a different atomic Dom; ⊥_Undef when the given result has no such atom, or when only a single (v, B) is given without source context')
bad = MTM(sites=('x', 'x'), s0=(0, 0), T=('x',), eps=Fr(0), t0=1, t1=1)
db = report(identify(Lab(bad), h, Decl()), Decl(), 'Q')
check(db.v == '⊥' and db.Code.startswith('⊥_Dom'), f'five-tuple of an invalid declaration: v = ⊥, Code = {db.Code}')
print()
print('all passed' if ok_all else 'some failed')
~~~
