A.6  field\_final.py  The F-layer evaluator of Appendix A.1 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* v1.0, verbatim identical to that paper's Appendix A.1; it computes the reference truth of the F encodings of known models

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A: F-layer (finite separable layer) evaluator.
Formal simulation, not certification of material causation.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from math import isfinite
from collections import defaultdict
from fractions import Fraction
Tri = bool | None
def exact(v):
    """Each input number is taken as the exact rational given by its decimal representation; all F-layer arithmetic is carried out exactly over the rationals."""
    return Fraction(repr(v)) if isinstance(v, float) else Fraction(v)
def tri_and(*xs):
    if False in xs:return False
    if None in xs:return None
    return True
def tri_or(xs):
    xs=list(xs)
    if True in xs:return True
    if None in xs:return None
    return False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pulse:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; amount:float; external:bool=True; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Process:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; carrier_inside:bool; law:str
    reads:tuple[str,...]=(); coefficient:float=0.; bias:float=0.
    certified_effect:Tri=True; certified_read:Tri=True
    # rule: constant, linear (sum of read sites), bilinear (two sites)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Link:
    source:str; target:str; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spec:
    sites:tuple[str,...]; initial:tuple[float,...]; window:tuple[int,...]
    pulses:tuple[Pulse,...]; processes:tuple[Process,...]
    links:tuple[Link,...]=(); identity:Tri=True; trace_history:Tri=True
    closed_graph:bool=True; epsilon:float=1e-9
    declared_T:tuple[str,...]|None=None

def _num(v, nonneg=False):
    # Legal numbers: integers, rationals (Fraction), or finite decimal floats
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)): return False
    if isinstance(v, float) and not isfinite(v): return False
    return (not nonneg) or v >= 0
def check(x):
    if not x.sites or len(set(x.sites))!=len(x.sites):raise ValueError('sites')
    if x.declared_T is None or not x.declared_T or len(set(x.declared_T))!=len(x.declared_T) or any(s not in x.sites for s in x.declared_T):raise ValueError('declared T')
    if not x.window or tuple(range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+1))!=x.window:raise ValueError('window')
    if len(x.initial)!=len(x.sites) or not all(_num(z,True) for z in x.initial):raise ValueError('initial')
    if not _num(x.epsilon,True):raise ValueError('epsilon')
    if x.identity not in (True,False,None) or x.trace_history not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('identity')
    names=set()
    for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes):
        if not e.name or e.name in names:raise ValueError('event id')
        names.add(e.name)
        if e.step<x.window[0]-1 or e.step>x.window[-1]:raise ValueError('event time')
        if e.target not in x.sites:raise ValueError('target')
        if isinstance(e,Pulse):
            if not _num(e.amount,True):raise ValueError('pulse amount')
            # In F, certified is the certification of “whether the pulse actually occurs”: 1 = occurs, 0 = does not occur; ⊥ is outside the single-trajectory domain of F and must be enumerated by branch in R
            if e.certified is None:raise ValueError('F_UNDECIDED_PULSE: enumerate occurrence branches in R')
            if e.certified not in (True,False):raise ValueError('pulse evidence')
        else:
            if e.law not in ('constant','linear','bilinear'):raise ValueError('law')
            if any(s not in x.sites for s in e.reads):raise ValueError('read sites')
            if e.law=='bilinear' and len(e.reads)!=2:raise ValueError('bilinear arity')
            if e.law=='linear' and not e.reads:raise ValueError('linear reads')
            if e.law=='constant' and e.reads:raise ValueError('constant reads')
            if not _num(e.coefficient) or not _num(e.bias):raise ValueError('params')
            # D20: the action certification must be decided; a mechanism with undetermined action is outside the F domain (even when its component is zero)
            if e.certified_effect not in (True,False):raise ValueError('undecided mechanism effect')
            if e.certified_read not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('evidence')
    # All trace nodes are snapshots at integer t; process nodes at integer t+0.5.
    # Strict temporal order prevents fabricated same-step read-after-write.
    times={f'state:{s}:{t}':float(t) for s in x.sites for t in range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+2)}
    times.update({e.name:float(e.step)+0.5 for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes)})
    for link in x.links:
        if link.source not in times or link.target not in times:raise ValueError('link endpoint')
        if times[link.source]>=times[link.target]:raise ValueError('noncausal link')
        if link.certified not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('link evidence')
    return times
def path_status(links,starts,target,closed=True):
    adjacency=defaultdict(list)
    for l in links:adjacency[l.source].append(l)
    def reachable(allow):
        seen=set(starts);queue=list(starts)
        while queue:
            u=queue.pop()
            for l in adjacency[u]:
                if allow(l.certified) and l.target not in seen:
                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # out-of-boundary cause: changes left at this position by certified, effective out-of-boundary action components (pulses, or processes whose carrier is outside the boundary)
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # Count only pulses certified as occurring and effective; a pulse confirmed not to occur contributes nothing and registers no formation or cause
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # Support for the organizational condition: the numerical law depends on the read positions, or the process is a pure read (component not an effective change, |c| ≤ ε); read certification enters as a separate conjunct, and an undetermined value is not truncated to false in advance
            # Functional support is decided by a mathematically nonzero partial derivative (coefficient and partner reading exactly nonzero), not conflated with the effective-change threshold ε
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # Formation of a new element: with its carrier inside B it is formation by in-field continuation; with its carrier outside the boundary it is formation by signal
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # An effective component of a process whose carrier is outside the boundary is a change left by a signal, whether the element is new or old and whether the component is positive or negative
                if not m.carrier_inside:
                    signal_marks[m.target].append((m.name,t))
            elif is_action is None: # defensive check: an undetermined action certification is already excluded from the domain by check()
                raise ValueError('unresolved effect cannot determine numeric trajectory')
            if t in x.window:
                if m.carrier_inside:part.append(is_cond)
                hdyn.append(tri_and(m.target in active_T,is_action,is_hist))
            events.append({'id':m.name,'delta':value,'act':is_action,'cond':is_cond,'histcond':is_hist,'route':route})
        for s in x.sites:
            new=before[s]+sum(v for _,v in contributions[s])
            # Depth must not be negative: an exact updated value below zero leaves the domain; no truncation to zero, no tolerance
            if new < 0:raise ValueError('negative trace depth')
            state[s]=new
            if state[s]>eps and formation[s]:history_snapshots[s].append(t+1)
        if t in x.window:reports.append({'step':t,'before':before,'after':dict(state),'contributions':dict(contributions),'events':events})
    ts=x.declared_T
    eligible=all(state[s]>eps and formation[s] for s in ts)
    sources={origin for site in ts for origin,t in signal_marks[site]}
    trace=tri_and(bool(sources),eligible,x.identity,x.trace_history)
    pw=tri_or(part); hw=tri_or(hdyn)
    return {'trace':trace,'part':pw,'hdyn':hw,'Q':tri_and(trace,pw,hw),'reports':reports,'physical_certificate':False}
~~~

A.7  hosts.py  Host Instance A: the minimal representative model of the MLP candidate and the sources of each of its conditions (7.5(3))

~~~python
"""The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field, Appendix A.7: host instance: the minimal representative model of the MLP candidate (single-weight linear unit, squared loss, online gradient).
Level L is the algorithmic level: the program is the adjudicated host, and the model mode has no Dom4 gap at this level; a hardware implementation is a separate instance and is not adjudicated here.
Three instances:
  χ_ext  training, optimizer carrier outside the boundary (B = weight and forward read-out);
  χ_in   training, optimizer carrier inside the boundary (B extended to include the optimizer; a separate adjudication instance);
  χ_frz  frozen deployment (no training input within W).
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from mtm import Mech, MTM, Lab, truth, trajectory, identify, net, dep0, formation, full_decl, selfloop_noext
def sl(res, decl):
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: None}[selfloop_noext(res, decl)[0]]
ETA = Fr(1, 4); TARGET = Fr(2); EPS = Fr(1, 100)
ok_all = True
def check(c, msg):
    global ok_all
    print(('  pass  ' if c else '  fail  ') + msg); ok_all = ok_all and c
def unit(opt_inside):
    # weight w: written at the entry beat by an out-of-boundary initialization pulse (signal); the forward read-out fwd is a pure-read process inside B (faithful read-out);
    # optimizer opt: under input 'a' (one training sample, x = 1, target 2), Δw = η(2 − w), i.e. the linear law b = 2η, k = −η; it does not run under other inputs.
    fwd = Mech('fwd', 'w', True, 'linear', ('w',), Fr(0), Fr(0))
    opt = Mech('opt', 'w', opt_inside, 'linear', ('w',), -ETA, ETA * TARGET, (('rest', 0), ('init', 0)))
    return MTM(sites=('w',), s0=(0,), T=('w',), eps=EPS, t0=1, t1=3,
               pulses=(('init', (('w', 1),)),), mechs=(fwd, opt))
def y1_verdicts(M, hist, f_class=False, complete=False):
    """Section 4.5. Y₁ only: q can only be adjudicated 0; Part and HDyn are adjudicated 0 only when every position of T stays at most ε throughout W; everything else is ⊥_Prof.
    With an F-class declaration (f_class): q can be adjudicated 1 by threshold crossing. With a complete position inventory (complete, EF1): the degenerate negative determination of Fld."""
    traj = trajectory(M, hist); end = traj[-1]
    q = {}
    for x in M.T:
        if end[x] <= M.eps: q[x] = 0
        elif f_class and any(st[x] <= M.eps for st in traj): q[x] = 1
        else: q[x] = '⊥_Prof'
    inW = traj[1:len(traj) - 1]          # pre-beat states of beats t₀ … t₁
    lowT = all(st[x] <= M.eps for st in inW for x in M.T)
    lowAll = all(st[x] <= M.eps for st in inW for x in M.sites) or all(end[x] <= M.eps for x in M.sites)
    ph = 0 if lowT else '⊥_Prof'
    return {'q': q, 'r': '⊥_Prof', 'Part': ph, 'HDyn': ph, 'Fld': 0 if (complete and lowAll) else '⊥_Prof'}
def lemma10(M, hist):
    """Lemma 10 (SAM, LOC): at some beat within W the events acting on w are exactly one process and no pulse, and Dep₀ and the net change are effective."""
    traj = trajectory(M, hist); form = formation(M, hist)
    for idx, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        if t < M.t0: continue
        s = traj[idx]
        acting = [m for m in M.mechs if m.target == 'w' and m.runs(t, u) and m.name != 'fwd']
        pulsed = any(tg == 'w' for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u))
        if len(acting) == 1 and not pulsed and s['w'] > M.eps and any(f < t for f in form['w']):
            if dep0(M, t, s, u, 'w', 'w', s['w'], s['w'] + 1) and abs(net(M, t, s, u)['w'] - s['w']) > M.eps:
                return 1
    return '⊥_Prof'
train = ['init', 'a', 'a', 'a']
frozen = ['init', 'rest', 'rest', 'rest']
X_ext, X_in = unit(False), unit(True)
import inspect
print('H0  Sources of the conditions for Instance A (7.5(3))')
print('  Three kinds: [definition] conditions the model has by definition (the program text is the host, algorithmic level); [intervention] conditions obtained by actually performing an intervention or computation on the simulation bench; [flag] given only by the Boolean declarations of full_decl.')
print('  The Booleans of full_decl only hand conditions to the adjudicator and prove nothing; every item below is also borne by [definition] or [intervention].')
lab = Lab(X_ext); trj = trajectory(X_ext, train)
def sep_mod_ok(M, hist):
    """SEP: net change = the effective pulses + the effective components; MOD: knocking out m removes exactly m's component. Checked beat by beat along the actual trajectory."""
    tr_ = trajectory(M, hist)
    for idx, (t, u) in enumerate(zip(M.beats(), hist)):
        st = tr_[idx]; full_ = lab.full(t, st, u)
        tot = {x: Fr(0) for x in M.sites}
        for tg, a in M.pulses_of(u): tot[tg] += a if a > M.eps else 0
        for m in M.mechs:
            if not m.runs(t, u): continue
            c = m.value(st, u); c = c if abs(c) > M.eps else Fr(0)
            tot[m.target] += c
            if full_[m.target] - lab.ko(t, st, u, m.name)[m.target] != c: return False
        if any(full_[x] - st[x] != tot[x] for x in M.sites): return False
    return True
check(sep_mod_ok(X_ext, train) and sep_mod_ok(X_ext, frozen), 'SEP, MOD [definition + intervention]: knocking out each process beat by beat along the training and frozen trajectories, the net change is exactly the sum of the effective components, and a knockout removes exactly that component')
check(lab.side is None and lab.set_local(trj[1], 'w', 3) == {**trj[1], 'w': 3},
      'LOC (incl. exclusivity) [intervention]: a local setting of w changes only w; the evidence edges of HistCond come from the settings that identify actually executes (H1, second item)')
check(len(trj) == len(X_ext.beats()) + 1, 'REC [definition]: the record covers every beat from t₀ − 1 to t₁ (trajectory length = number of beats + 1)')
check(all(trj[0][x] <= X_ext.eps for x in X_ext.sites), 'ENT [intervention]: at the start of the record every position is at most ε (w = 0); no formative history before the start awaits registration')
~~~

check(identify(lab, train, full\_decl(X\_ext))\['unexplained'\] is False,

~~~text
      'INV-a [definition], INV-c [definition]: the inventory consists of all processes in the program; the closure check leaves no residual (Lemma 8: necessary, not sufficient; INV-c is borne by the program text)')
fwd_t = next(t for t, u in zip(X_ext.beats(), train) if t >= X_ext.t0)
o1 = lab.out(fwd_t, trj[1], train[1], 'fwd'); o2 = lab.out(fwd_t, {**trj[1], 'w': trj[1]['w'] + 1}, train[1], 'fwd')
check(o1 == (trj[1]['w'],) and o1 != o2,
      'OUT-f (fwd) [intervention]: the read-out equals the value of the read position w, and setting w changes the read-out (position-wise injective); fwd reads no other position (read-position set = {w})')
check(list(inspect.signature(X_ext.mechs[1].runs).parameters) == ['t', 'u'] and all(m.law in ('constant', 'linear', 'bilinear') for m in X_ext.mechs),
      'EF4 [definition]: whether a process runs depends only on the beat and the input (the parameters of runs include no state), and components depend on the positions read only through F-class numeric laws; in no case do the Trace-Responses determine occurrence, timing or position')
print('  BEAT, POS, EF1–EF3, EF5 [definition]: discrete beats; positions are the state-variable names; level L is the program itself. Hardware or any material realization is a separate instance and is not adjudicated here.')
print('  IMP [definition]: the numeric laws of fwd and opt read directly the state variable w that set_local rewrites, so the setting is the very value the original process reads; this does not extend to any material realization.')
print('H1  Training instance χ_ext (optimizer outside the boundary)')
print('  trajectory w:', [str(st['w']) for st in trajectory(X_ext, train)])
tr = truth(X_ext, train); D = full_decl(X_ext); idr = identify(Lab(X_ext), train, D, detail=True)
check(tr['Q'] is True and idr['Q'] is True, 'Y₂ (SEP, MOD, LOC, BEAT, INV-a, INV-c, OUT-f) adjudicates Fld = 1, agreeing with the F-layer evaluator')
check(('w@1', 'opt@1') in idr['edges'], 'the booked term of opt varies with the local setting of w: evidence edge (w,1)→opt certified as 1, HistCond = 1')
check(sl(idr, D) == 0 and sl(idr, full_decl(X_ext, inv_c=False)) is None,
      'in-boundary self-feedback type: under INV-c and EF4 the only running process in B, fwd, has Act = 0, so the quantifier domain has no candidate; adjudicated 0; without INV-c, ⊥')
v = y1_verdicts(X_ext, train)
check(v == {'q': {'w': '⊥_Prof'}, 'r': '⊥_Prof', 'Part': '⊥_Prof', 'HDyn': '⊥_Prof', 'Fld': '⊥_Prof'},
      f'Y₁ only: every duty ⊥_Prof ({v})')
check(y1_verdicts(X_ext, train, f_class=True)['q'] == {'w': 1}, 'Y₁ with F-class declaration: w crosses the threshold within the record, q = 1')
print('H2  Training instance χ_in (optimizer inside the boundary; a separate adjudication instance)')
tr = truth(X_in, train); D = full_decl(X_in); idr = identify(Lab(X_in), train, D, detail=True)
check(tr['Q'] is True and idr['Q'] is True, 'Y₂ adjudicates Fld = 1, agreeing with the F-layer evaluator')
check(sl(idr, D) == 1, 'in-boundary self-feedback type: opt in B has both Act = HistCond = 1; there is no out-of-boundary component, and the response is unchanged under external-clamp LOC; adjudicated 1')
check(lemma10(X_in, train) == 1, 'Y₁ with SAM, LOC: Lemma 10 adjudicates HDyn = 1')
print('H3  Frozen deployment χ_frz')
tr = truth(X_ext, frozen); idr = identify(Lab(X_ext), frozen, full_decl(X_ext))
~~~

check(tr\['hdyn'\] is False and idr\['hdyn'\] is False and idr\['Q'\] is False and idr\['part'\] is True,

~~~text
      'Y₂: Part = 1 (the forward read-out runs on w); no process acts on w within W, HDyn = 0; under INV-c and EF1–EF5 (holding at the algorithmic level), Fld = 0')
v = y1_verdicts(X_ext, frozen)
check(v['HDyn'] == '⊥_Prof' and v['Fld'] == '⊥_Prof',
      'Y₁ only: w exceeds ε within W, so HDyn and Fld cannot be adjudicated 0 (the twin of Theorem 5(2)); adjudicated ⊥_Prof')
r2 = identify(Lab(X_ext), frozen, full_decl(X_ext, inv_c=False))
check(r2['hdyn'] is None, 'when the inventory is not declared complete, HDyn is adjudicated ⊥_Inv, not 0')
print()
print('all passed' if ok_all else 'some failed')
~~~

## Appendix B  Run Results

Runtime environment: Python 3.13.17. All five run commands exit with code 0.  
Reference run times (Python 3.13.17): layer1.py about 5 s, layer1\_second.py about 90 s, t3cd.py about 2 s, experiments.py about 12 s, hosts.py under 1 s. layer1\_second.py includes a brute-force enumeration up to history length 13 (about 25 million history-pair prefixes); when re-running it, set the timeout above 5 minutes.

Correspondence between outputs and main text: B.1 corresponds to the exact rational arithmetic of 3.3, the twin protocol, beat-wise independent disturbances and the augmented declaration for cross-beat correlation, transition-kernel validity, the cut-off declaration of the structural representation, the supported domain and marginal TV, and the separate listing of the disagreement probability; the monitor automaton for the set of legitimate histories and the 𝓗 check of the historical form Act\_𝓟^h; the declaration entry of 4.2; the operational configuration of Theorem 4 (same Prof, changing r or k flips the verdict); the in-range 0 and whole-history 0 of 4.3; the resolution threshold of Dom2 and its domain; the declared probe class of Act\_𝓟, the clause (b) threshold and the cut-reading timing; the two Dep thresholds and the setting domain; the five-tuple report of 4.1; the instances of Theorems 1 and 2 and Lemmas 1–7; and 7.5(1). B.2 corresponds to the models M1–M4 of Theorem 1 (a2)(b), Theorem 2 (b) and Lemma 2; B.3 to Theorem 2 (c)(d). C1–C17 of B.4 correspond, in order, to Theorem 5(1), Theorem 6, Lemma 8 with E20–E22 of Section 6, Lemma 9, Lemmas 10 and 12, Theorem 5(2)(3), Theorem 6(2), Theorem 6(3), 4.6, Theorem 7(2), 4.2 ENT with Theorem 6(4)(B), Theorem 6(4)(B), 4.8, the perturbation coverage of 4.6 with Theorem 6(2), Lemma 6.1, the mechanism-level declaration entry of 4.2, and the five-tuple reports of 4.1 and 4.9. In B.5, H0 corresponds to the sources of the conditions of Instance A in 7.5(3), and H1–H3 to χ\_ext, χ\_in, χ\_frz of Instance A in 7.5(3).  
Verification Index  
This index lists the readings and sources of the model instances and finite verifications cited in the main text, so that each can be traced item by item. Finite verifications are not universal proofs. The second-layer verifications take the F encoding of a known model as reference truth (Appendix A.6) and show that Y₂ inversion reproduces that encoding; they are not material certification (see the note on evidential strength after Theorem 6).  
1\. First layer (Appendix B.1, B.2, B.3)  
Correlated bits: joint-TV Tr^rest = 1, while the maximum single-variable marginal difference is 0; after block re-encoding it is still 1.  
Conserved pair, mirrored storage: Tr^rest = 1.  
Echo: Tr = 0.  
Mutual write-back to external store: unclamped Tr = 1; clamped Tr = 0 (universal); Act\_𝓟 = 0.  
Random out-of-boundary: with only the transition kernel given, clamping cannot be realized, ⊥\_Obs; once the structural representation is declared, the twin protocol makes it adjudicable: the difference passes only through the out-of-boundary component and vanishes under clamping, Tr = 0.  
Signal not entering the system (the counterexample of 3.3): with the same history and shared disturbances, the marginal TV of y and the disagreement probability are both 0; with the same history and independent sampling, both are 0.5; with different histories and shared disturbances, both are 0; under the twin protocol Tr = 0. A model with no out-of-boundary component has, under the same history and independent sampling, marginal TV 0 but disagreement probability 0.5.  
Set of legitimate histories: the full-sequence language has a witness, but when 𝓗 forbids w the verdict is 0; when 𝓗 requires w after a, the shortest full-sequence witness (history length 1) is forbidden, and the shortest legitimate witness has history length 2.  
Act\_𝓟 clause (b): the spontaneous flow channel gives 1 at r\_act = 0.5 and 0 at r\_act = 5.  
Structural representation of the clamped mixed state (C = 3): the twin protocol and value clamping agree in verdict and witness.  
Operational configuration: for the same random-write transition kernel, changing only r or k flips Tr (note after Theorem 4).  
Cross-beat disturbances: for two models with the same single-beat marginal, one beat-wise independent and one fully correlated across beats, the marginal TV of the history pair (w, w) vs (rest, w) is 0 and 0.5 respectively.  
Transition-kernel validity: kernels summing to 0.8, with a negative probability, with an empty distribution, or with a single-point probability of 0.3 are all ⊥\_Dom under both exact adjudication and enumeration; a disturbance distribution summing to 0.9 is ⊥\_Dom; deterministic transitions containing zero-probability entries are adjudicated as usual.  
Dep with an infinite setting domain: ⊥\_Dom when a measured level does not belong to V\_y, or when only “infinite” is written without a membership test.  
Structural representation and supply cut-off: with a structural representation plus a separate cut-off kernel (cut\_φ missing), Tr^cut and Hist^cut are ⊥\_Undef, while Tr^rest is still 1; once cut\_φ is supplied, Tr^cut has the valid value 0.  
Historical form and 𝓗: when 𝓗 allows w and rest but forbids the probe p, Act\_𝓟^h is ⊥\_Dom; when 𝓗 imposes no constraint, the same 𝓟 gives 1; when the actual history does not belong to 𝓗, ⊥\_Dom.  
Declaration entry: q = −1, k = 0, H\_max = 0, r\_act \< 0, a malformed monitor automaton, as well as an empty or duplicated position name, an initial-state length mismatch and an alphabet lacking rest, all give ⊥\_Dom.  
Resolution threshold: mirrored storage gives Tr = 1 at r = 0.05 and at r = 0; r = 1 and r = −0.1 lie outside Dom2 and give ⊥\_Dom.  
Two Dep thresholds: on a model whose two effect differences are both 1, (r\_μ, r\_ρ) = (2, 0.5), (2, 1.5), (0.5, 1.5) give 1, 0, 1 respectively.  
Clamped mixed state M1: Tr = 1, shortest history length 12, N = 2, \|I′\| = 13, bound 28; the witness depends on the mixed state (E18).  
Long preparatory history M2 (n = 6): enumeration with H\_max = 6 gives 0; exact adjudication gives 1, shortest history length 7.  
Position-bound counterexample M3: clamped, k = 1 is universally 0 and k = 2 gives 1 (depends on the mixed state (1, 1)); unclamped, k = 1 and 2 are universally 0.  
Gated clock: a witness exists at k = 3 positions; random write: at r = 0.08, k = 1 gives 0 and k = 2 gives 1.  
Medium leakage: Tr^rest and Tr^cut are 1 at q = 6 and 0 at q = 300.  
Refresh-maintained charge: Tr^rest = 1, Tr^cut = 0; Hist^rest = 1, Hist^cut = 0.  
Undeclared supply cut-off: Tr^cut is ⊥\_Undef.  
Fuse ROM: starting from blank with a record, Hist^cut = Hist^rest = 1; starting from an already-programmed state without a record, Hist is ⊥\_Obs.  
Spontaneous flow channel: act already differs before the probe, and clause (b) gives Act\_𝓟 = 1. For the same model and the same history w vs rest: 𝓟 = {rest} gives 0, 𝓟 = {p} gives 1, and 𝓟 consisting of all length-1 probes gives 1; an undeclared 𝓟 gives ⊥\_Undef; with 𝓟 = {rest} the adjudication procedure does not apply p.  
Receiver saturation: Act\_𝓟 = 1. Self-read saturation: Act\_𝓟 = 0. Read-out potentiation (no spontaneous activity): Act\_𝓟 = 1 (clause (a)).  
Probe legitimacy checked first: when 𝓗 forbids w and 𝓟 = {w}, both the prospective and the historical form give ⊥\_Dom; a legitimate empty 𝓟 gives 0; a legitimate 𝓟 = {rest} without a witness gives 0.  
History segments and override parameters: a segment index that is out of range, negative, of the wrong type or duplicated, a start-state dimension mismatch, a Dep variable index out of range, and an input not in the alphabet all give ⊥\_Dom; a legitimate segment gives 1 as usual.  
Identity of membership tests: different implementations have different digests and different Dom; repeated declarations of the same implementation have the same Dom; referencing a module global value without a declared identifier gives ⊥\_Dom, and once the identifier is declared it is recorded in Dom.  
Lemma 2: the copy in mirrored storage is retained under rest, exact Tr = 1; a copy cleared by rest does not satisfy the retention condition, exact Tr = 0.  
Exact thresholds: when TV equals exactly r = 1/3 or 1/7, Tr is 0, and at r = 1/3 − 10⁻¹² it is 1; the same holds for the structural representation (twin protocol) and for Hist; Act\_𝓟 clause (a) gives 1 at r = 1/4 and 0 at r = 1/3.  
Phased run of the cut reading: Hist^rest = Hist^cut = 1; Act\_𝓟^h is 1 under the rest reading and 0 under the cut reading (the probe is applied to the state after supply cut-off).  
Monitor-extended bound: when 𝓗 accepts only histories of length exactly 20, the shortest witness has history length 20, not exceeding the bound found by search, 21²·B = 2646 (an a priori bound requires declared upper bounds on N and \|S\_in\|, which were not provided); with an empty accepting set Tr = 0; when 5 rests are required first, the shortest witness has history length 6.  
Lemma 7 (redundant formation): Hist((a, b) vs (rest, b)) = 0, although the first beat a of the actual history changes x from 0 to 1.  
Cut-off sentinel: an ordinary input named \_\_cut executes under the transition law, and both the transition-kernel path and the structural-representation path give Tr^rest = 1; under genuine supply cut-off (clearing), Tr^cut = 0.  
Cut reading, clause (b): when p writes persistently outside the declared block, Act\_𝓟^h under the cut reading is 0; the spontaneous flow channel gives 1 under the cut reading.  
Membership-test digests include keyword-only default values: allowed = True and allowed = False give different Dom; repeated declarations with the same frozen default have the same Dom.  
Actual histories given as tuples and as lists are equivalent: Hist, Act\_h and Dep give the same values, and the reports have the same Dom.  
Exact Dep readings: when the mean difference is exactly 9/10, the model with floating-point readings gives 0 at r\_μ = 9/10, 1 slightly below it and 0 slightly above it, the same as the all-Fraction model; with pre-state 0.2 and post-state 0.3, Δμ is exactly 1/10; for a multi-point distribution Δμ = 1/20, ρ = 1.  
Floating-point act readings: when the increment difference is exactly r\_act = 1/10, clause (b) gives 0, and 1 slightly below it; decimal-literal readings equal Fraction readings; the model's own floating-point arithmetic (0.1·3) is interpreted by the value it produces.  
Effective model representation: the start 0.1 and Fraction(1, 10) are both the rational 1/10; enumeration and exact adjudication agree in value and shortest history length, and explicit and default starts agree; exogenous disturbances call φ and cut\_φ with the declared keys, and the net transition, twin protocol and cut-off structure agree.  
Raw probabilities validated first: negative entries give ⊥\_Dom whether merged by state key or pushed through φ; merging same-state successors with positive probabilities proceeds as usual.  
Invalid exogenous disturbance distributions at the declaration entry: four distributions (negative probability, sum not equal to 1, empty distribution, non-finite values), both with the restricted legitimate histories (rest*, no distinct history pair) and with the full sequence, give ⊥\_Dom at the entry, in Tr enumeration and exact adjudication, in Act, in Hist and in the report; a valid distribution gives 0 under rest*.  
Cross-verification: on 12 deterministic models, enumeration and exact adjudication agree in value and in shortest witness history length.  
Dep setting levels and a finite V\_y are compared in the same effective representation: declared {1/10, 1} and measured {0.1, 1} do not give ⊥\_Dom, and take the same value as the all-Fraction notation.  
Dep setting domain: for the model in which, under rest, Δx = 1 if and only if z = 1 and y ≥ 3, with V\_y infinite and only {0, 1, 2} measured, the verdict is in-range 0; with 3 included in the measurement it is 1; with the finite V\_y = {0, 1, 2} fully enumerated it is 0.  
Receiver saturation, linear additive: Dep = 1, 0 respectively; a random final state gives Dep ⊥\_Obs.  
Five-tuple report: mirrored storage gives Tr = 1 (witness), mutual write-back gives exact Tr = 0 (full-graph search), and Dep with an infinite domain gives in-range 0 and reports the measured subdomain; the reason code of ⊥ enters Code; Dom records the effective configuration, positional and keyword arguments give the same Dom, default values are recorded as well, and changing q, r, k, H, 𝓟 or 𝓗 changes Dom accordingly.  
2\. Second layer (Appendix B.4)  
C1 five twin pairs and their ∃T Q; C2 mechanism-level reconstruction, 600 instances; C3 protocol removal; C4 T\_max reduction, 600 instances; C5 conditional bridging and counterexamples; C6 twin construction, 300 instances, and threshold-crossing formation at 600 positions; C7 local response and functional support; C8 pure read and OUT-f; C9 certified and uncertified evidence edges; C10 unfaithful encoding; C11 pre-start formative history and ENT; C12 antecedents of the whole-history negative value; C13 negative determination of the in-boundary self-feedback type; C14 perturbation coverage; C15 load-bearing character of the negative-determination antecedent list (class by class over nine verdict classes); C16 mechanism-level declaration entry; C17 five-tuple report and ⊥ reason codes (including the same-source configuration of the atomic reports). 79 verifications in all; see Appendix B.4. The second-layer verifications use the F-layer evaluator of Appendix A.1 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* to compute the reference truth of the F encodings of known models (Appendix A.6); this comparison demonstrates that the inversion reproduces the known F encodings, and is not material certification (see the note on evidential strength after Theorem 6).  
Theorem 6(2)(A) and (4): 20000 random processes; over 170994 legitimate perturbations of effective processes the conclusion holds without exception, including 12600 same-position bilinear cases and the δ = −s boundary; among 60276 perturbations of ineffective processes there are 1631 threshold-crossing responses, all with support (C7 in Appendix B.4). The reconstructions of 600 mechanism-transition-model instances are compared item by item with the F-layer evaluator: Trace agrees; HDyn agrees, or is ⊥\_Obs because the applied default setting takes a one-step successor out of the F domain so that no legitimate response is obtained (5 cases); Part and Q do not exceed the true value in the information order, and Q is ⊥ in 20 cases (C2).  
Lemma 6.1: 300 instances, each with the same test bench, the same input history and the same T held fixed, and only the declaration changed. Across the nine classes Form, Mark, Act, Cond, HistCond, Trace, Part, HDyn, Q there are 3659 verdicts of 0 in all; their antecedents are removed one at a time, 5829 removals in all, and after each removal the verdict is no longer 0. Of these, Trace, Part, HDyn, Q account for 709 verdicts and 1295 removals. When no declaration is supplied, only the Y₁ verdict that consumes no antecedent (not retained at window end) remains (C15 in Appendix B.4).  
Lemma 9: 600 random instances, 476 of them with nonempty T\_max (C4).  
Lemma 10: 1501 random (beat, x, y) instances, equal case by case (C5).  
Lemma 12: 800 random instances (C5).

&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  20000 random processes: over 170994 perturbations of effective processes, response ⇔ i ∈ Supp\_act (including 12600 same-position bilinear ones and the δ = −s boundary); of 60276 perturbations of ineffective processes, 1631 gave threshold-crossing responses, all with i ∈ Supp\_act  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  critical: c(s) = ε (ineffective); after δ = 1 the threshold is crossed, the response is 1, and x ∈ Supp\_act; here only the dependence is issued as 1, Act stays 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  critical, reversed: after δ = −1/2 still subthreshold, the response is 0, yet x ∈ Supp\_act — no response from an ineffective process gives only ⊥, never 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  same-position bilinear at s(x) = 0: the finite difference is nonzero while the D15 partial derivative is zero — the criterion must be restricted to available positions (s \> ε ≥ 0)  
C8  Pure read: OUT-f and ⊥\_Sub  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  zero component, same trajectory, one actually reads x and the other reads nothing: true Part is 1 for one, 0 for the other  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  read-out decoupled from reading (constant output): the two instances have the same public observations; Part is ⊥\_Sub for both, and neither direction is issued  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  under OUT-f: the read-out is the value of the positions actually read; Part is 1 for one, 0 for the other, matching the truth  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  no read-out declared: Part of the zero-component process is ⊥\_Sub  
C9  Certified and uncertified evidence edges  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  reachable certification: the LOC setting of y changes the booked term of m → evidence edge (y,1)→m set to 1, read certification 1, HistCond = 1, matching the truth  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  uncertified: even with a declared certified link (y,1)→m, the constant-law m shows no response → functional support empty, HistCond = 0 (D18); path closure is not relied on  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  uncertified (LOC exclusivity fails): setting x also changes y, producing the spurious edge (x,1)→m — a numeric response certifies a path only under LOC exclusivity  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  inventory not declared complete: HDyn is ⊥\_Inv, and 0 is not issued  
C10  Without encoding fidelity, Q\_F(T\_max) = 0 does not read back as ¬Fld  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  the F encoding gives Q\_F = 0 (including T\_max = {x, y}), while the LOC response to y is 1: occurrence is decided by the Trace-Response, so HDyn holds on the host — negative determination requires the universal encoding fidelity of 8.2(6), and this host is not an F-class declaration  
C11  Formative history before the record start: ENT and ⊥\_Rec  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  ENT declared (the entry beat faithfully registers the formative history before the start): a position present at the start with no formation event has no formation; TraceSet = 0, consistent with the F layer  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  ENT not declared: Form and Mark are ⊥\_Rec, not 0; availability is undetermined, so HistCond and HDyn depending on that position are also ⊥  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  once the pre-start signal write is faithfully registered as an entry-beat event: formation and trace-leaving can be certified, Q = 1  
C12  Negative values of Form, Mark and Trace require INV-c, REC, ENT  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  out-of-boundary write hidden while INV-c is falsely declared: Mark is misjudged as 0, TraceSet is misjudged as 0 — a 0 for Mark requires INV-c  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  INV-c not declared: Mark is ⊥, TraceSet is ⊥  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  REC not declared: HistEv is ⊥\_Rec, TraceSet is ⊥  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  INV-c, REC and ENT all declared: TraceSet = 1, consistent with the F layer  
C13  In-boundary self-feedback type: “no certified doubly true process” does not imply 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  a process in B with Act = 1 and HistCond = ⊥ (formation of the read position y undetermined): the self-feedback type is ⊥, not 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  the same process with the formation of y registered: HistCond = 1, the self-feedback type is 1 — a 0 in the previous case would be wrong  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  B holds only a pure read (Act = 0) and the actor is outside the boundary: with INV-c and EF4 the value is 0; without INV-c or EF4 it is ⊥  
C14  Perturbation coverage: applied without response, no legitimate perturbation, not applied  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  legitimate perturbation applied: a response occurs, HistCond = 1, matching the truth  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  the declared setting domain for y holds only the current value (no legitimate nonzero perturbation): not 0 but ⊥  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  a legitimate perturbation exists but was not applied: ⊥  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  one-sided setting domain (decrease only): one legitimate perturbation suffices, HistCond = 1  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  constant-law process: 0 when every candidate position has been perturbed with no response; ⊥ when some candidate position cannot be perturbed  
C15  Antecedent list of every 0 verdict: with the observational facts fixed, withdrawing any one item ends the 0 verdict  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Form: 197 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 685 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Mark: 221 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 741 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Act: 960 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 960 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Cond: 967 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 1306 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  HistCond: 605 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 842 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Trace: 167 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 287 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Part: 118 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 242 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  HDyn: 190 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 434 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Q: 234 verdicts of 0; their antecedents withdrawn one at a time, 332 withdrawals in all, and none still gives 0; every antecedent belongs to the listed declaration items  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  random 300 cases in total: 3659 verdicts of 0, 5829 withdrawals (of these, Trace, Part, HDyn and Q together account for 709 verdicts and 1295 withdrawals); observational facts, candidate positions and event sets fixed

C16  Invalid mechanism-level declarations do not enter evaluation: ⊥, with the reason reported  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  valid declaration: passes the entry point and is evaluated as usual  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  family of erroneous declaration parameters (ε negative, duplicate position name, empty position names, process lacks an action target, process run beat outside the declared window, read position not in the position set, t₀ \> t₁, input history length mismatch, perturbation is 0, invalid perturbation value, perturbation position not in the position set, negative trajectory depth (constant shallowing −1 from 0), linear law with empty read positions, constant law with read positions, declared T duplicated): all give ⊥\_Dom with a reason, and no unexplained exception is thrown; consistent with the rejections of the upstream evaluator

&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  legitimate shallowing is evaluated as usual: no response to the +1 perturbation gives 0; the applied −1/2 makes the one-step successor negative, so it is not a legitimate perturbation and no legitimate response was obtained: ⊥\_Obs (D\_x is not inferred to be empty)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  perturbation causes kept apart: the applied default +1 is illegitimate (successor −1): ⊥\_Obs, and D\_x is not reported empty; the applied −1/2 is legitimate, HistCond = 1; D\_x declared empty: ⊥\_Pert; exists but not applied: ⊥\_Obs  
C17  Five-tuple reports of second-layer verdicts and ⊥ reason codes

&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  five-tuple of a verdict 0: Code lists the antecedents (0: antecedents INV-c, PROTO)

&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Dom records the effective configuration: T, ε, declared period, input history, perturbation setting and test bench; a different perturbation setting gives a different Dom ((('y', '-1/2'),) vs default +1 at every position)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  ineffective process (\|component\| ≤ ε) with no response: the Code of HistCond = ⊥\_Sub  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  atom reports and composite reports share a source: the Dom of Act, Form, Mark and the in-boundary self-feedback type all carry the configuration of the same adjudication, and Read records the source ({'type': 'Act', 'object': ('m', 1)}); a different perturbation setting gives a different atomic Dom; ⊥\_Undef when the given result has no such atom, or when only a single (v, B) is given without source context  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  five-tuple of an invalid declaration: v = ⊥, Code = ⊥\_Dom: position name empty or duplicated  
all passed

B.5  Run command: python3 hosts.py  
H0  Sources of the conditions for Instance A (7.5(3))  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Three kinds: \[definition\] conditions the model has by definition (the program text is the host, algorithmic level); \[intervention\] conditions obtained by actually performing an intervention or computation on the simulation bench; \[flag\] given only by the Boolean declarations of full\_decl.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;The Booleans of full\_decl only hand conditions to the adjudicator and prove nothing; every item below is also borne by \[definition\] or \[intervention\].  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  SEP, MOD \[definition + intervention\]: knocking out each process beat by beat along the training and frozen trajectories, the net change is exactly the sum of the effective components, and a knockout removes exactly that component  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  LOC (incl. exclusivity) \[intervention\]: a local setting of w changes only w; the evidence edges of HistCond come from the settings that identify actually executes (H1, second item)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  REC \[definition\]: the record covers every beat from t₀ − 1 to t₁ (trajectory length = number of beats + 1)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  ENT \[intervention\]: at the start of the record every position is at most ε (w = 0); no formative history before the start awaits registration  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  INV-a \[definition\], INV-c \[definition\]: the inventory consists of all processes in the program; the closure check leaves no residual (Lemma 8: necessary, not sufficient; INV-c is borne by the program text)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  OUT-f (fwd) \[intervention\]: the read-out equals the value of the read position w, and setting w changes the read-out (position-wise injective); fwd reads no other position (read-position set = {w})  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  EF4 \[definition\]: whether a process runs depends only on the beat and the input (the parameters of runs include no state), and components depend on the positions read only through F-class numeric laws; in no case do the Trace-Responses determine occurrence, timing or position  
&nbsp;&nbsp;BEAT, POS, EF1–EF3, EF5 \[definition\]: discrete beats; positions are the state-variable names; level L is the program itself. Hardware or any material realization is a separate instance and is not adjudicated here.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;IMP \[definition\]: the numeric laws of fwd and opt read directly the state variable w that set\_local rewrites, so the setting is the very value the original process reads; this does not extend to any material realization.  
H1  Training instance χ\_ext (optimizer outside the boundary)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;trajectory w: \['0', '1', '5/4', '23/16', '101/64'\]  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Y₂ (SEP, MOD, LOC, BEAT, INV-a, INV-c, OUT-f) adjudicates Fld = 1, agreeing with the F-layer evaluator  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  the booked term of opt varies with the local setting of w: evidence edge (w,1)→opt certified as 1, HistCond = 1  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  in-boundary self-feedback type: under INV-c and EF4 the only running process in B, fwd, has Act = 0, so the quantifier domain has no candidate; adjudicated 0; without INV-c, ⊥  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Y₁ only: every duty ⊥\_Prof ({'q': {'w': '⊥\_Prof'}, 'r': '⊥\_Prof', 'Part': '⊥\_Prof', 'HDyn': '⊥\_Prof', 'Fld': '⊥\_Prof'})  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Y₁ with F-class declaration: w crosses the threshold within the record, q = 1  
H2  Training instance χ\_in (optimizer inside the boundary; a separate adjudication instance)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Y₂ adjudicates Fld = 1, agreeing with the F-layer evaluator  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  in-boundary self-feedback type: opt in B has both Act = HistCond = 1; there is no out-of-boundary component, and the response is unchanged under external-clamp LOC; adjudicated 1  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Y₁ with SAM, LOC: Lemma 10 adjudicates HDyn = 1  
H3  Frozen deployment χ\_frz  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Y₂: Part = 1 (the forward read-out runs on w); no process acts on w within W, HDyn = 0; under INV-c and EF1–EF5 (holding at the algorithmic level), Fld = 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  Y₁ only: w exceeds ε within W, so HDyn and Fld cannot be adjudicated 0 (the twin of Theorem 5(2)); adjudicated ⊥\_Prof  
&nbsp;&nbsp;pass  when the inventory is not declared complete, HDyn is adjudicated ⊥\_Inv, not 0  
all passed
